In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Higher-Order vs. Quadratic Binary Optimization: Which Is Better for Probability Optimization with Tensor Sampling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26206)
*Hadi Salloum,Kirill Novoselov,Aleksandr Pochtarev,Yaroslav Kholodov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文比较了HUBO和QUBO两种模型在基于张量采样的概率优化中的表现，发现对于包含高阶交互的问题，原生HUBO公式更优，而QUBO在自然二次问题上仍有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究HUBO和QUBO模型在概率优化中的相对优势，为实践者选择优化框架提供指导。

Method: 结合理论分析（公式表达能力和约简开销）与实证实验（合成多项式目标、RSA分解和Max-Cut实例）。

Result: 原生HUBO在处理高阶交互问题时更优，因为约简为QUBO会增加张量采样器看到的有效维度；QUBO在自然二次问题上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 选择优化框架时需在模型保真度、维度增长和求解器性能之间权衡，为不同场景提供了实用建议。

Abstract: This article explores the comparative strengths of Higher-Order Unconstrained Binary Optimization (HUBO) and Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) models in the context of probability optimization using tensor sampling techniques. HUBO can represent interactions beyond pairwise terms without introducing auxiliary variables, while QUBO benefits from a simpler algebraic structure and a wide range of established solvers. We combine a theoretical analysis of formulation expressiveness and reduction overhead with empirical experiments on synthetic polynomial objectives, RSA factorization, and Max-Cut instances. The results show that native HUBO formulations are often preferable when the original problem contains high-order interactions, because reducing such problems to QUBO increases the effective dimension seen by tensor samplers. At the same time, QUBO remains competitive for naturally quadratic problems. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting an optimization framework that balances model fidelity, dimension growth, and solver performance.

</details>


### [144] [Input Convex Neural Network as a Surrogate in Stability-Constrained Optimization for IBR-dominated Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26446)
*Wangkun Xu,Hongyang Jia,Yi Wang,Ning Zhang,Fei Teng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文指出了输入凸神经网络（ICNN）在电力系统优化中作为代理模型时的两个常见公式化局限：一是使用通用Big-M混合整数重构会引入不必要的辅助二进制变量；二是反转稳定性不等式会将凸子水平集变为非凸超水平集，破坏基于割集方法的全局收敛保证。针对这些问题，提出了基于LP的精确epigraph重构、全局收敛的外逼近方案以及保持可行性的内逼近方案，并在IEEE 14和118节点机组组合实例上进行了仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICNN越来越多地用作电力系统稳定性指标的代理模型并嵌入优化约束，但现有公式化方法存在两个常见局限，可能抵消ICNN凸性带来的优势。

Method: (i) 针对ReLU-ICNN提出基于线性规划（LP）的精确epigraph重构方法；(ii) 在子水平集约定下提出具有全局收敛保证的外逼近方案；(iii) 针对超水平集约定提出保持可行性的内逼近方案。

Result: 在IEEE 14节点和118节点机组组合实例上进行仿真，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 澄清了ICNN在电力系统优化中应用时的两个关键公式化局限，并提供了相应的改进方案，确保凸性优势得以充分利用。

Abstract: Input convex neural networks (ICNNs) are increasingly used as surrogates for stability indices and embedded as constraints in power-system optimization. This letter clarifies two recurring formulation limitations that can negate ICNN convexity benefits: (i) applying generic Big-$M$ mixed-integer reformulations introduces auxiliary binaries that are unnecessary for enforcing ICNN sublevel constraints; and (ii) reversing the stability inequality transforms a convex sublevel set into a generally nonconvex superlevel set, invalidating global-convergence guarantees of cut-based methods. After clarifying the limitations, we provide (i) an exact LP-based epigraph reformulation for ReLU-ICNNs, (ii) an outer-approximation scheme with global guarantees under the sublevel convention, and (iii) a feasibility-preserving inner-approximation scheme for the superlevel convention, with simulations on IEEE 14- and 118-bus unit commitment instances.

</details>


### [145] [Minkowski-Type Wasserstein Metrics and Barycenters for Location-Scale Mixtures with Application to Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26482)
*Songyan Luo,Yunxin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限位置-尺度混合模型的离散最优传输框架，通过将连续多边缘OT问题简化为混合组件间的离散传输问题，将计算复杂度从二次降至线性，并在VisDA-C基准上验证了其在降低计算成本和内存占用同时保持竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散最优传输依赖经验测度间的点匹配，计算成本随样本量至少呈二次增长，亟需更高效的替代方案。

Method: 基于广义Minkowski不等式定义特定函数类，刻画多变量位置-尺度族间的OT映射；在可识别性假设下将Wasserstein型度量和重心扩展到混合模型；通过限制联合耦合为特定混合结构，将连续多边缘OT问题降维为混合组件间的离散传输问题。

Result: 计算复杂度从二次降至线性；在VisDA-C基准上实现了与经验OT方法相当的精度，同时显著降低了计算成本和内存占用。

Conclusion: 所提框架为混合模型下的最优传输提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案，验证了在分布对齐等实际任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Discrete optimal transport (OT) typically relies on pointwise matching between empirical measures, incurring computational costs that scale at least quadratically with the sample size. To circumvent this limitation, we introduce a mathematical framework for OT between finite location-scale mixture models. By defining a specific function class grounded in generalized Minkowski inequalities and characterizing OT maps between multivariate location-scale families, we extend Wasserstein-type metrics and barycenters to these mixture models under the assumption of identifiability. Furthermore, we prove that restricting joint couplings to a specific mixture structure reduces the continuous multimarginal OT problem to a discrete transport problem over mixture components. Computing transport plans between these components rather than individual samples reduces the computational complexity to linear scaling with respect to the sample size. Empirical evaluations on the VisDA-C benchmark confirm that this strategy achieves competitive accuracy compared to existing empirical OT approaches, while substantially reducing the computational cost and memory footprint.

</details>


### [146] [Mean-Field PhiBE: Continuous-Time Mean-Field Reinforcement Learning from Discrete-Time Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26498)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Martin Hernandez,Qinxin Yan,Yuhua Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间平均场控制问题，在仅能获取离散时间转移数据且群体动力学遵循未知McKean-Vlasov SDE的设定下，提出了一种Mean-Field-PhiBE（MF-PhiBE）框架，通过将离散时间转移信息融入Wasserstein空间上的连续时间PDE中，结合策略梯度定理，实现了无模型的演员-评论家方法，并证明了其一阶和二阶（LQ情形）精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型驱动平均场控制中，策略评估依赖于McKean-Vlasov HJB方程中的漂移和扩散系数，但这些系数在仅有离散时间数据时不可识别；而直接采用时间离散Bellman方程虽避免了不可识别问题，却丢失了微分方程结构。为此需要桥接两种视角，在保持连续时间PDE生成元结构的同时利用离散数据。

Method: 提出MF-PhiBE，将离散时间一步估计量替换策略评估方程中未知的无穷小漂移和协方差，保留McKean-Vlasov HJB的生成元结构；推导熵正则化随机反馈策略的策略梯度定理，通过动作-wise无穷小优势与策略得分表达演员方向；结合二者构建无模型演员-评论家方法。

Result: 证明了最优MF-PhiBE策略诱导的值函数逼近连续时间最优值的误差为O(Δt)一阶一致性；在线性二次型（LQR）情形下仅需单步数据即可达到二阶精度；在LQR基准和人群厌恶问题上的数值实验验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: MF-PhiBE框架有效弥合了连续时间PDE方法与离散时间数据之间的鸿沟，为仅含离散观测的连续时间平均场控制提供了可理论保证的无模型求解方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses model-free continuous-time mean-field control in a setting where the population dynamics evolve continuously according to an unknown McKean-Vlasov stochastic differential equation, while only discrete-time transition data are available. In the model-based formulation, policy evaluation is naturally described by a stationary Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation on $\mathcal P_2(\mathbb R^d)$, but this equation involves the drift and diffusion coefficients of the controlled McKean-Vlasov dynamics, which are not identifiable when only discrete-time data are available. On the other hand, a direct reduction to a time-discrete Bellman equation avoids the non-identifiability issue but loses the differential equation structure. To bridge these two viewpoints, we introduce a Mean-Field-PhiBE (MF-PhiBE), which incorporates discrete-time transition information into a continuous-time PDE on the Wasserstein space. The MF-PhiBE replaces the unknown infinitesimal drift and covariance in the policy-evaluation equation by one-step estimators computed from data, while preserving the generator structure of the McKean-Vlasov HJB equation. We also derive a policy-gradient theorem for entropy-regularized randomized feedback policies, expressing the actor direction through an action-wise infinitesimal advantage and the score of the policy. Combining these two ingredients yields a model-free actor-critic method. We prove a first-order consistency estimate showing that the value induced by an optimal MF-PhiBE policy approximates the optimal continuous-time value with an error of order $Δt$. In the linear-quadratic case, we show our approximation achieves second-order accuracy with only one-step data. Numerical experiments on an LQR benchmark and a crowd-aversion problem illustrate the proposed framework.

</details>


### [147] [Maximizing Throughput in an M/G/1 Queue with Customer Abandonments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26544)
*Runhua Wu,Hayriye Ayhan,Douglas G. Down*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有顾客放弃行为的单服务器队列中的最优服务器分配策略问题。在泊松到达和指数分布耐心时间设定下，当服务时间服从Erlang-K或超指数分布且决策者可观察服务阶段时，最短剩余期望处理时间（SREPT）策略能最大化长期平均吞吐量，且与放弃率无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有顾客放弃行为的排队系统中如何分配服务器资源以最大化长期平均吞吐量，特别是在服务时间可观测的情况下寻找最优策略。

Method: 论文考虑泊松到达和指数分布耐心时间的单服务器排队系统，分析服务时间服从Erlang-K或超指数分布的情形，假设决策者能观察顾客的服务阶段信息，研究SREPT策略的性能。

Result: 证明了SREPT策略在Erlang-K和超指数分布服务时间下能最大化长期平均吞吐量，且该最优性与顾客放弃率无关。

Conclusion: 在特定服务时间分布下，SREPT策略是最优的服务器分配策略，其最优性对放弃率具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of identifying the optimal server assignment policy in single-server queues with customer abandonment. We consider a system with Poisson arrivals and exponentially distributed patience times. We show that when service times follow either an Erlang-$K$ or a hyperexponential distribution and the decision maker can observe the phase of a customer's service time, the Shortest Remaining Expected Processing Time (SREPT) policy maximizes the long-run average throughput, independent of the abandonment rate.

</details>


### [148] [Three-Objective Integral R2 Subset Selection: NP-Hardness and Submodular Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26591)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究三目标优化中基于积分R2指标的代表点选择问题：证明了该指标下集合函数具有单调子模性，从而贪心选择可保证(1-1/e)的近似比；同时证明了精确固定基数子集选择是NP-hard问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标优化中，从有限的Pareto前沿近似中选择固定数量的代表点是一个基础的后处理任务，本文针对三目标情形下的积分R2指标研究该问题。

Method: 理论上：证明积分R2改进量是单调子模集函数，从而贪心算法有(1-1/e)的近似保证；通过透视变换将问题映射到带权锚定盒并集问题，利用三维锚定盒构造证明NP-hardness。算法上：提出基于细分法的贪心选择实现，最坏情况时间复杂度O(n^6)。

Result: 正面：贪心选择至少能达到最优(1-1/e)比例的R2间隙缩减；负面：精确固定基数子集选择在三目标下是NP-hard的，将可解的两目标情形与难解的三目标情形区分开来。

Conclusion: 三目标积分R2代表点选择问题虽精确求解是NP-hard的，但可利用子模优化原理获得有理论保证的近似解，为实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Selecting a fixed number of representative points from a finite Pareto-front approximation is a fundamental post-processing task in multiobjective optimization. This paper studies this problem for the integral R2 indicator in three objectives, where the indicator is defined as the integral of the lower envelope of weighted Tchebycheff scalarizations over the two-dimensional weight simplex. We provide two complementary algorithmic results. On the positive side, we show that the integral R2 improvement with respect to any fixed baseline is a monotone submodular set function. For the usual ideal-point based R2 indicator, with the ideal point fixed, this yields a direct gap-reduction guarantee: greedy selection closes at least a $(1-1/e)$-fraction of the maximum possible R2 gap between a fixed dominated anchor value and the best cardinality-$k$ value. We also give a tested greedy implementation that evaluates exact integral R2 values by subdivision, with worst-case running time $O(n^6)$. On the negative side, we prove that exact fixed-cardinality subset selection is NP-hard already in three objectives. The hardness proof uses a perspective transformation that maps Tchebycheff-shadow improvements to a weighted anchored-box union problem with density $(x_1+x_2+x_3)^{-4}$, and then adapts the three-dimensional anchored-box construction of Bringmann, Cabello, and Emmerich. Together, these results separate the tractable two-objective case from the three-objective case while identifying a principled approximation route based on submodular optimization.

</details>


### [149] [Comparative Analysis of Linear Battery Models for Carbon Emission Optimization in Solar Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26648)
*Anna Sacilotto,Ambrogio Maria Bernardelli,Stefano Gualandi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对住宅光伏-电池储能系统(PV-BESS)在不确定性下的碳排放最小化问题，开发并比较了从基于规则的自消耗启发式到完全随机线性反馈控制的一系列线性优化模型。基于主成分分析生成的大规模场景集，实验表明直接随机优化（程序化电池模型）显著优于启发式控制，接近理论下限，而基于反馈的模型泛化能力并未提升但计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决住宅PV-BESS系统在负荷、太阳能发电和电网碳强度存在随机波动的情况下，如何最小化等效碳排放的问题，并评估不同复杂度优化策略的减排效果。

Method: 构建并比较了具有不同预期度和反馈复杂度的线性优化模型层级结构（从规则启发式到完全随机线性反馈控制），通过主成分分析从真实运行数据生成大规模场景集进行随机优化。

Result: 直接随机优化（程序化电池模型）的减排效果接近理论下限（全知电池基准），显著优于启发式控制；基于反馈的模型在训练性能上略有提升但在未见数据上泛化能力未增强，且计算成本更高。

Conclusion: 线性随机规划为分布式PV-BESS系统的排放感知能量管理提供了有效且可处理的理论框架。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of minimizing equivalent carbon emissions in residential photovoltaic-battery energy storage systems (PV-BESS) under uncertainty. We develop and compare a hierarchy of linear optimization models that differ in their degree of anticipativity and feedback complexity, ranging from a rule-based self-consumption heuristic to fully stochastic formulations with linear feedback control. The proposed models explicitly incorporate the stochastic variability of household load, solar production, and grid carbon intensity through large scenario sets generated via principal component analysis of real operational data. Computational experiments on synthetic yet realistic scenarios show that direct stochastic optimization of expected emissions (Programmed Battery model) substantially outperforms heuristic control, achieving emission reductions close to the theoretical lower bound provided by the Omniscient Battery benchmark. Feedback-based models marginally improve training performance but do not generalize better on unseen data, while incurring higher computational costs. Overall, results demonstrate that linear stochastic programming provides an effective and tractable framework for emission-aware energy management in distributed PV-BESS systems.

</details>


### [150] [Sinkhorn-Knopp balancing with generalised martingale-type constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26739)
*Abigail Langbridge,Martin Corless,Robert Shorten*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义熵正则化最优传输（Optimal Transport）框架，用于解决存在一般运输约束和异构灵活性边际约束的资源分配问题，并设计了可证明收敛的Sinkhorn类算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输方法在处理具有一般运输约束和异构灵活性边际约束的资源分配问题时存在局限，尤其是在共享经济等涉及运输计划实现和鞅研究的实际场景中，需要更通用的求解框架。

Method: 将经典熵正则化最优传输问题进行推广，引入一般运输约束和异构灵活性边际约束；设计并证明其收敛性的Sinkhorn类迭代算法。

Result: 提出了可证明收敛的Sinkhorn-like算法，并通过示例验证了该方法的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该广义框架和算法能够有效处理具有复杂约束的资源分配问题，拓展了熵正则化最优传输方法的应用范围。

Abstract: We consider the problem of optimally distributing resources from a set of suppliers to a set of consumers in the presence of general transportation constraints, and including heterogeneous flexibility in the marginal constraints. Such problems frequently arise in a variety of practical settings; for example, in the context of sharing economy applications, where one is not only interested in the transportation plan, but also its realisation, and in other problems that involve the study of martingales. Our principal contribution in this paper is to consider a generalisation of the classic entropically regularised Optimal Transport formulation in which such problems can be solved with a Sinkhorn algorithm. In particular, we present provably convergent Sinkhorn-like algorithms for solving this class of problems, and provide examples to both illustrate the utility of our approach as well as its efficacy.

</details>


### [151] [Second-order Methods for Multiobjective Composite Optimization: Preconditioning Strategies, Subspace Variants and Inexact Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26792)
*Jian Chen,Xinmin Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对多目标复合优化问题中光滑分量病态条件导致的数值求解困难，提出了一种预条件近端Barzilai-Borwein方法。该方法结合目标级BB缩放与共享预条件子，并开发子空间变体避免非对角度量近端映射，在非凸设定下证明全局收敛性和线性收敛率，数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标复合优化在稀疏正则化、约束多目标模型和多任务学习中广泛存在，但现有方法存在两难：近端梯度法每轮迭代代价低但病态条件下收敛慢；近端牛顿/拟牛顿法利用曲率信息但需计算昂贵的度量近端映射。为突破这一困境，需要设计一种兼顾低迭代代价与快速收敛的新方法。

Method: 提出预条件近端Barzilai-Borwein方法：(1) 目标级BB缩放减少目标间不平衡；(2) 公共预条件子捕获共享曲率信息；(3) 子空间变体将搜索方向限制在近端梯度方向与投影历史方向张成的二维子空间中，通过构造关于预条件度量的共轭基将子空间模型分解为可解的一维子问题；(4) 扩展至含g_i(Ax)形式的非光滑项，利用线性算子感知的预条件子通过对偶子问题实现显式近端计算；(5) 基于松弛下降条件分析非精确版本。

Result: 在非凸设定下建立了非精确算法的全局收敛性，并在误差界条件下证明了线性收敛率。在病态ℓ1正则化、结构化ℓ1正则化和线性约束问题上的数值实验表明该方法有效。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持近端梯度法低迭代代价的同时，通过曲率信息加速收敛，有效解决了多目标复合优化中病态光滑分量带来的挑战，并为更复杂的非光滑项结构提供了处理框架。

Abstract: Multiobjective composite optimization problems arise in sparse regularization, constrained multiobjective models, and multi-task learning, but their numerical solution remains challenging when the smooth components are ill-conditioned. Proximal gradient methods are inexpensive per iteration but may converge slowly, while proximal Newton and quasi-Newton methods exploit curvature information at the cost of evaluating expensive metric proximal mappings. To address these issues, we propose a preconditioned proximal Barzilai--Borwein method for multiobjective composite optimization. The method combines objective-wise Barzilai--Borwein scaling, which reduces imbalance among objectives, with a common preconditioner that captures shared curvature information. To avoid non-diagonal metric proximal mappings, we develop a subspace variant in which the search direction is computed in a two-dimensional subspace generated by a proximal-gradient-type direction and a projected historical direction. By constructing a conjugate basis with respect to the preconditioning metric, the subspace model decomposes into tractable one-dimensional subproblems. The framework is further extended to nonsmooth terms of the form $g_i(Ax)$ through a linear-operator-aware preconditioner, yielding explicit proximal evaluations via dual subproblems. We also analyze an inexact version based on relaxed descent conditions. We establish the global convergence of the inexact algorithm in the nonconvex setting and prove a linear convergence rate under an error-bound condition. Numerical experiments on ill-conditioned $\ell_1$-regularized, structured $\ell_1$-regularized, and linearly constrained problems demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [152] [Separation, Constraint Qualifications, and Cycling in Outer Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26897)
*Erik Tamm,Jan Kronqvist*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究外逼近算法中两个常被忽视的收敛性假设——约束规范性和子问题精确求解——指出它们与循环问题的关联，并提出使用扩展切割平面作为回退策略，在放松假设下实现了有限收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外逼近算法是求解凸混合整数非线性规划的经典方法，但其收敛性所依赖的两个关键假设（约束规范性成立、非线性规划子问题可精确求解）在文献中鲜有讨论，且在实际中可能被违背导致算法失效。

Method: (1) 理论分析约束规范性失效和子问题近似求解如何导致分离失败及循环现象；(2) 证明当Slater条件接近被违反时，存在靠近精确解的点使得分离失败；(3) 提出在检测到循环时采用扩展切割平面作为回退策略，并证明该策略在放松假设下仍能保证有限收敛。

Result: 揭示了约束规范性与子问题精确度不足是引发循环的根本原因；证明了Slater条件接近违反时的分离失败现象；提出的扩展切割面回退策略在放松的约束规范性假设下实现了算法的有限收敛。

Conclusion: 扩展切割平面策略有效泛化了外逼近算法的收敛理论，使其在更宽松的假设条件下仍能有限收敛，增强了算法的鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: The outer approximation algorithm is a widely used method for solving convex mixed-integer nonlinear programs. While the algorithm is well established in theory and practice, certain assumptions underlying its convergence are rarely discussed in the literature. In this paper, we examine two such assumptions: that a constraint qualification holds at the optimal solution of each nonlinear programming subproblem, and that these subproblems can be solved exactly. We argue that both assumptions are connected to the issue of cycling, by which we mean that the same integer assignment reappears in successive iterations of the algorithm. When a constraint qualification fails, separation of the current iterate from the feasible set is not guaranteed, which can cause the algorithm to stall. When the nonlinear programming subproblem is solved only approximately, separation may likewise fail, and we show that high precision can be required in certain cases. To formalize the connection between these issues, we prove that when Slater's condition is nearly violated, a point close to the exact solution can be found at which separation fails. Furthermore, within the outer approximation algorithm, we propose to use extended cutting planes as a fallback strategy when cycling is detected. We prove that this approach yields a finitely convergent algorithm under relaxed assumptions regarding constraint qualifications, thereby generalizing the convergence theory of the outer approximation algorithm.

</details>


### [153] [The parental parsimony problem on binary, tree-child phylogenetic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26919)
*Martin Frohn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对系统发育网络中的亲本简约性评分问题（PPS），首次提出了常数因子近似算法（适用于任意固定叶标签及一类NP-hard网络），并引入了一种针对二叉树-子网络的高效精确求解算法，在模拟数据上验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育重建是计算生物学的主要挑战之一。在系统发育网络的多种重建方法中，存在一个关键子任务：给定网络上的叶标签，寻找网络内最简约的树。该子任务因网络所捕捉的不同生物模型假设（如杂交、重组、异源多倍体、不完全谱系分选）而有不同变体。本文聚焦于同时包含上述多种进化现象的场景，即PPS问题，该问题为NP-hard组合优化问题，亟需有效的近似与精确求解方法。

Method: 本文针对二元、半单纯形、树-子系统发育网络（PPS仍为NP-hard的一类网络），提出了首个常数因子近似算法（适用于任意但固定的叶标签）。同时，针对二元、树-子系统发育网络，设计了一种新的精确求解算法，并在模拟数据上进行性能分析。

Result: 首次为PPS问题提供了常数因子近似保证；针对二元树-子网络提出了新的精确算法，并在模拟数据集上评估了其表现。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了PPS问题在近似算法方面的空白，同时提供了可实际求解的精确算法，为系统发育网络中更复杂的进化场景分析提供了理论和方法基础。

Abstract: Phylogenetic reconstruction is one of the major challenges in computational biology. Among existing reconstruction methods for phylogenetic networks, an important subtask emerges in extending a leaf-labelling on a phylogenetic network to determine a most parsimonious tree inside the network. There exist different variants of this subtask depending on the biological model assumptions for which distinct evolutionary phenomena are captured by the network. In this article we assume that next to hybridization or recombination events, also allopolyploidy or incomplete lineage sorting are present. Then, finding the most parsimonious tree inside the network is called the parental parsimony score problem (PPS), a NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem. We provide the first constant-factor approximation for the PPS on arbitrary but fixed leaf labels and a class of networks on which the PPS remains NP-hard, namely binary, semi-simplex, tree-child phylogenetic networks. Furthermore, we introduce a novel exact solution algorithm for the PPS on binary, tree-child phylogenetic networks and analyze its performance on simulated data.

</details>


### [154] [Numerical Approximation for Path-Dependent McKean-Vlasov Control with Non-Asymptotic Error Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27181)
*Olivier Bokanowski,Jean-Francois Chassagneux,Xinyu Li,Christoph Reisinger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对路径依赖型McKean-Vlasov控制问题，提出了一个统一逼近与学习框架，包括Euler离散化、动态规划原理、粒子系统近似和神经网络策略梯度算法，并给出了非渐近误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径依赖型McKean-Vlasov控制模型描述了大量相互作用的智能体，其动态和成本具有历史依赖性。该问题在连续时间、开环控制框架下缺乏统一的逼近和学习方法。

Method: 1) 采用分段常数控制的Euler离散化方案，得到O(h^{1/4})的非渐近误差；2) 建立离散动态规划原理，证明开环控制与历史依赖反馈控制的价值等价性；3) 引入相互作用粒子系统逼近连续时间值函数，总体误差为O(h^{1/4})+O(M^{-γ})；4) 提出基于路径特征的神经网络策略梯度方法。

Result: Euler离散化误差为O(h^{1/4})，粒子系统逼近的总体误差为O(h^{1/4})+O(M^{-γ})（γ>0明确给出），数值实验（包括路径依赖线性二次基准问题）验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架首次为连续时间路径依赖MKV问题提供了可实现的逼近与学习算法，理论误差界与数值结果一致，证明了方法的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Path-dependent McKean--Vlasov (MKV) control models large interacting populations with history-dependent dynamics and costs. This paper develops a unified approximation-and-learning framework for continuous time path-dependent MKV problem under open-loop controls. First, an Euler discretization scheme with piecewise-constant controls is shown to achieve a non-asymptotic error of $O(h^{1/4})$. Second, we establish a discrete dynamic programming principle and prove value equivalence between open-loop and history-dependent feedback controls, enabling optimization on a reduced filtration. Third, an interacting particle system is introduced to approximate the continuous-time value, yielding an overall error bound of $O(h^{1/4}) + O(M^{-γ})$ for $M$ particles and an explicitly given $γ> 0$. Finally, we propose a fully implementable neural-network policy-gradient method using pathwise features. Numerical experiments, including a path-dependent linear-quadratic benchmark, demonstrate the effectiveness of the algorithm.

</details>


### [155] [Benign Landscape of Quadratic Programs with Orthogonality Constraints and Its Application to Heteroscedastic Probabilistic PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27189)
*Peng Wang,Po Chen,Rujun Jiang,Laura Balzano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带正交约束的齐次二次规划（QPOC）的优化景观，证明其每个临界点要么是全局最大值点，要么是严格鞍点；并将该理论应用于异方差概率PCA（HePPCA），证明其群体版本具有良性优化景观，且样本版本在样本量足够大时以高概率继承这些性质，为基于收缩的优化方法实现全局解的局部线性收敛提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究QPOC的优化景观特征，并利用该理论解释HePPCA的优化行为，为实际优化方法（如收缩法）的收敛性提供理论保证。

Method: 首先对QPOC建立临界点集的闭式刻画，推导全局最优性的充要条件，证明每个非最优临界点具有正曲率方向；随后证明HePPCA群体版本是QPOC的特例，并进一步证明其局部测地线强凹性；最后通过概率分析证明样本版本以高概率继承这些性质。

Result: QPOC的优化景观是良性的：所有临界点要么是全局最大值点，要么是严格鞍点；HePPCA群体版本具有良性景观且在全局最大值点附近满足局部测地线强凹性；样本版本在大样本下以高概率继承这些性质。

Conclusion: 该理论为基于收缩的优化方法在QPOC和HePPCA上实现全局解的局部线性收敛提供了理论支撑，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: In this work, we study the optimization landscape of homogeneous quadratic programs with orthogonality constraints (QPOC) and apply the resulting theory to heteroscedastic probabilistic PCA (HePPCA). For QPOC, we establish a complete characterization of the benign optimization landscape by showing that every critical point is either a global maximizer or a strict saddle point. Our analysis builds on a closed-form characterization of the critical point set, from which we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for global optimality and show that every non-optimal critical point has a direction of positive curvature. As an application, we show that the population version of HePPCA is a special instance of QPOC and therefore has a benign optimization landscape; moreover, it satisfies local geodesic strong concavity near every global maximizer. We furthermore prove that, when the sample size is sufficiently large, the sample version of HePPCA inherits these favorable properties with high probability. Together with existing theory on avoiding strict saddle points, our results provide a theoretical justification for the observed local linear convergence of retraction-based optimization methods to global solutions for both QPOC and HePPCA. Finally, we present numerical experiments to corroborate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [156] [Optimizing Accessibility and Maximum Travel Times in Transit Networks: The Hub Covering Location Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27196)
*Carmen-Ana Domínguez-Bravo,Elena Fernández,Armin Lüer-Villagra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化模型，用于支持综合交通系统设计决策，通过混合整数线性规划和数学启发式方法，平衡用户短途出行时间与公共交通网络效率，以改善城市交通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改善城市交通流动性，通过优化综合交通系统设计来支持决策制定。

Method: 提出两种混合整数线性规划（MILP）公式和一种数学启发式（matheuristic）程序，并通过计算实验评估其效率。

Result: 计算实验揭示了求解结构的特征，并分析了各参数对系统设计的影响。

Conclusion: 该优化模型能有效支持综合交通系统设计决策，通过约束短途和长途出行时间分别保障用户可达性与系统有效性。

Abstract: This work introduces an optimization model to support decision-making in the design of integrated transit systems, with the aim of improving urban mobility. We assume that daily user trips consist of two types of segments: short segments where users apply their own transport between their homes or workplaces and the public transit access points, and a longer segment on the transportation network. Users' accessibility is ensured by imposing an upper limit on the travel times of the short segments. We assume that only users within a given coverage radius will employ the system. On the other hand, system effectiveness is ensured by limiting travel time on the transportation network. Two mixed-integer linear programming formulations and a matheuristic procedure are proposed, and their efficiency is evaluated through computational experiments. The results allow us to study the structure of the solutions obtained and analyze the effects of the parameters considered.

</details>


### [157] [Conjecture About Arbitrary Even-Order Convexity of $w$-Optimization of the Split CIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27260)
*Hao Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出关于w-优化问题具有任意偶数阶凸性的猜想，该问题源于数据融合中的分裂协方差交集滤波器（Split CIF），已知其具有二阶和四阶凸性，而猜想进一步认为其具有更高偶数阶凸性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: w-优化问题是从工程数据融合工具——分裂协方差交集滤波器（Split CIF）中抽象出的数学问题。作者基于观察和分析，提出该问题可能具有任意偶数阶凸性的猜想，旨在激发系统性的数学研究。

Method: 作者通过分析w-优化问题已知的二阶凸性（常规凸性）和四阶凸性，基于观察提出猜想：该问题具有任意偶数阶凸性（即2k阶凸性，k为正整数）。论文详细阐述了该猜想。

Result: 已知w-优化问题具有二阶凸性和四阶凸性，这保证了Split CIF的快速实现。作者提出该问题可能具有任意偶数阶凸性的猜想。

Conclusion: 虽然更高阶凸性对实际应用意义不大，但该猜想有望激发有价值的系统性数学技术研究。

Abstract: The $w$-optimization problem is a mathematical problem abstracted from a useful tool for general data fusion rooted in various engineering tasks. Purely from the perspective of practical applications for this useful tool namely the split covariance intersection filter (Split CIF), it is sufficient to know that the $w$-optimization problem enjoys not only the second-order convexity (namely convexity in conventional sense) but also the fourth-order convexity, thanks to which a guaranteed fast implementation of the Split CIF can be realized. On the other hand, based on certain observations and analysis, the author proposes a conjecture concerning the $w$-optimization problem for further mathematical study. The conjecture is that the $w$-optimization problem has arbitrary even-order convexity, or in other words, it has the second-order convexity, the fourth-order convexity, the sixth-order convexity, and so on -- As mentioned above, whether the $w$-optimization problem has higher-order convexity or not might be of little interest itself for practical applications, yet the author believes that the conjecture would stimulate study on systematic mathematical techniques that are potentially interesting and valuable -- This paper presents the conjecture in details.

</details>


### [158] [A Verification Theorem for an Optimal Control Problem Governed by the Convective Brinkman--Forchheimer Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27312)
*Sagar Gautam,Manil T. Mohan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二维和三维环面上由对流Brinkman-Forchheimer方程控制的最优控制问题，建立了验证定理，推导了庞特里亚金最大值原理，并在二维、三维超临界（r∈(3,5]）及临界（r=3,2βμ≥1）情形下严格证明了反馈表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体控制问题中，针对Navier-Stokes型模型的验证定理和反馈表征研究相对较少，而CBF系统要求与二维Navier-Stokes方程本质不同的正则性框架，因此需要建立新的验证理论。

Method: 基于强解理论，对非线性吸收项进行精细处理，在负阶Sobolev空间中建立新估计，并在更强拓扑下获得连续依赖性估计；在二维和三维超临界（r∈(3,5]）及临界（r=3,2βμ≥1）情形下构造验证框架。

Result: 成功推导了Pontryagin最大值原理，建立了二维和三维CBF系统的验证定理与反馈表征，特别在三维超临界和临界情形下实现了严格证明。

Conclusion: 本文建立的验证框架在二维和三维中均严格成立，为CBF系统的最优控制问题提供了完整的理论支撑。

Abstract: This article establishes a verification theorem for an optimal control problem governed by the two- and three-dimensional convective Brinkman--Forchheimer equations on the $d$-dimensional torus, $d\in\{2,3\}$:
  $$\frac{\partial\mathfrak{u}}{\partial t}
  -μΔ\mathfrak{u}
  +(\mathfrak{u}\cdot\nabla)\mathfrak{u}
  +α\mathfrak{u}
  +β|\mathfrak{u}|^{r-1}\mathfrak{u}
  +\nabla\mathfrak{p}
  =\boldsymbol{f},
  \qquad
  \nabla\cdot\mathfrak{u}=0,$$
  where $μ,α,β>0$ and $r\in[1,\infty)$. We derive the Pontryagin maximum principle and develop a verification framework for the associated control problem, a topic that has received comparatively little attention for fluid models of Navier--Stokes type. A major challenge in establishing the verification theorem and the corresponding feedback characterization for the CBF system is that the analysis requires a substantially different regularity framework from that used for the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations. In particular, the present approach relies on strong solution theory, a delicate treatment of the nonlinear absorption term, novel estimates in negative-order Sobolev spaces, and continuous dependence estimates in stronger topologies, especially in the three-dimensional setting. A distinctive feature of the present work is that the verification framework is developed not only in two dimensions, but also in the three-dimensional supercritical regime, corresponding to $r\in(3,5]$, and in the critical case $r=3$ under the condition $2βμ\geq1$. Consequently, the feedback characterization and verification arguments can be rigorously justified in both two and three dimensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Physics-guided Convolutional Neural Network for Domain Growth Prediction in Systems with Conserved Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26128)
*Vijay Yadav,Madhu Priya,Manish Dev Shrimali,Prabhat K. Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络作为代理模型，用于学习非线性偏微分方程描述的微观结构演化，以Cahn-Hilliard方程控制的相分离过程为例，证明该模型在长时间演化中保持稳定准确，并符合Lifshitz-Slyozov畴生长规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数值求解非线性偏微分方程计算成本高，而深度神经网络代理模型作为高效替代方案日益受到关注，因此需要设计能准确预测微观结构演化的代理模型。

Method: 提出基于注意力机制（attention-based）的物理引导卷积神经网络（physics-guided CNN）作为代理模型，通过学习Cahn-Hilliard方程控制下的二元混合物相分离全过程时间演化进行训练。

Result: 训练后的代理模型在长时间展开中保持稳定且准确，适用于临界和非临界混合物；能保持演化过程中的混合组成；准确捕捉畴尺寸增长，与Lifshitz-Slyozov畴增长定律一致。

Conclusion: 该框架对具有守恒动力学的系统建模有效，并可扩展到其他复杂动力系统。

Abstract: The spatiotemporal evolution of many physical, chemical, and biological systems is described by nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep neural network-based surrogate models have gained increasing interest as efficient alternatives to computationally expensive traditional numerical solvers. In this work, we propose an attention-based, physics-guided convolutional neural network as a surrogate model to learn the microstructural evolution of such systems. We train the model to accurately predict the full time-evolution of phase separation in binary mixtures governed by the Cahn-Hilliard equation. We show that predictions from our trained surrogate model remain stable and accurate over long-time rollouts for both critical and off-critical mixtures and preserve the mixture composition throughout evolution. We also show that our model accurately captures the growth of domain size and is consistent with the Lifshitz-Slyozov domain-growth law. The prediction results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for modeling systems with conserved kinetics and can be extended to other complex dynamical systems.

</details>


### [160] [\chisao{}: A GPU-Native Parallel Optimizer for Multimodal Black-Box Functions via Convergence-Anticonvergence Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26164)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 χίσαο（CHISAO），一种GPU原生的种群优化器，通过收敛-反收敛振荡循环同时并行处理整个样本批次，在Simon Fraser University优化基准套件的所有42个函数上，在维度d∈{2,4,8,16,32,64}下实现100%的模式恢复，速度最高提升39倍，且在噪声环境下仍保持100%可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态黑箱函数的所有模式查找是优化、贝叶斯推断和科学计算中的基本挑战，现有方法（如basin-hopping、CMA-ES、多起点梯度下降）顺序运行，无法利用现代GPU硬件的 massive 并行能力。

Method: 提出χίσαο（CHISAO）算法：GPU原生种群优化器，同时运行整个样本批次，利用有意的收敛-反收敛振荡循环——达到真正峰值的样本被冻结（"stuck"）并保留，其余样本通过基于动量的反收敛和随机平滑梯度继续探索；通过两种互补策略（Repulse Monkey和Golden Rooster）实现自适应重播种以维持种群多样性。

Result: 在Simon Fraser University优化基准套件全部42个函数、维度d∈{2,4,8,16,32,64}下，CHISAO在CPU基线方法在d≥8时崩溃的最难多模态函数上实现100%模式恢复；在Michalewicz d=64上相比basin-hopping加速34倍，在Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid d=64（纯GPU红利）上加速39倍；在噪声σ_noise高达1.0时仍保持100%模式检测可靠性。

Conclusion: CHISAO是一种高效的GPU原生多模态优化方法，在模式恢复率和计算速度上显著超越CPU基线方法，对噪声具有鲁棒性，已作为开源Python包发布在PyPI上。

Abstract: Finding all modes of a multimodal black-box function is a fundamental challenge in optimization, Bayesian inference, and scientific computing. Existing approaches -- basin-hopping, CMA-ES, multistart gradient descent -- operate sequentially and cannot exploit the massive parallelism of modern GPU hardware. We introduce \chisao{} (\textbf{C}onvergence-\textbf{H}alt-\textbf{I}nvert-\textbf{S}tick-\textbf{A}nd-\textbf{O}scillate), a GPU-native population optimizer that runs an entire sample batch simultaneously and exploits a deliberate convergence-anticonvergence oscillation cycle to escape local traps while freezing confirmed modes. The structural move is asymmetric: samples that reach true peaks are frozen (``stuck'') and preserved, while the rest keep exploring via momentum-based anti-convergence and stochastically smoothed gradients. Adaptive reseeding via two complementary strategies (Repulse Monkey and Golden Rooster) maintains population diversity throughout. On all 42 functions of the Simon Fraser University optimization benchmark suite across dimensions $d \in \{2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64\}$, \chisao{} achieves \textbf{100\%} mode recovery where all CPU baselines collapse at $d \geq 8$ on the hardest multimodal functions, at up to \textbf{$34\times$} speedup over basin-hopping on functions where all methods succeed (Michalewicz $d=64$) and up to \textbf{$39\times$} on unimodal functions (Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid $d=64$, pure GPU dividend). All benchmarks evaluate the objective by value alone -- gradients come from finite differences -- so the reported speedups are a derivative-free worst case. Under substantial likelihood noise ($σ_{\mathrm{noise}}$ up to 1.0), mode detection remains 100\% reliable. The algorithm is available as a standalone open-source Python package on PyPI.

</details>


### [161] [Implementation of reinforcement learning in chemical reaction networks: application to phototaxis as curiosity-driven exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26168)
*Ruyi Tang,Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Colliaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最小认知视角出发，将单细胞藻类的趋光运动重新建模为基于信息驱动的感知-运动过程，利用部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）与化学反应网络常微分方程（CRN-ODEs）相结合，并通过逆强化学习（IRL）从实验轨迹中推断行为目标，揭示了游动-翻滚交替作为一种信息获取策略的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有趋光性模型将藻类运动描述为机械的刺激-响应游动-翻滚过程，忽略了生物体如何主动采样环境以减少感知不确定性。作者希望从最小认知视角出发，将导航问题重新定义为具有主观性、信息驱动的感知-运动过程。

Method: 1）构建POMDP框架，将环境变量设为隐藏状态，细胞通过无记忆贝叶斯更新维持最小内部状态；2）将内部动力学通过化学反应网络常微分方程（CRN-ODEs）实现，平衡朝向光定向与探索性重定向；3）引入生物物理观测过程用于光感受，并设计可化学计算的信息增益多项式界；4）使用逆强化学习（IRL）对30条衣藻实验轨迹进行行为目标推断，并与随机模拟算法（SSA）基线进行对比。

Result: 模型能够重现实验观察到的对齐光分布，效果与基于目标函数的SSA基线相当。游动-翻滚交替行为在本框架下自然涌现为一种信息获取策略：翻滚使细胞重定向以采样新的感知构型，从而解决感知模糊性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了细胞内生化网络如何支持自适应的信息寻求行为，为理解细胞层面的最小认知和主动感知提供了计算框架。

Abstract: Living systems navigate environments using noisy and incomplete sensory signals. In unicellular algae, phototaxis is often modeled as a mechanistic run--tumble process driven by stimulus--response rules. However, such descriptions overlook how organisms actively sample their environment to reduce sensory ambiguity. From a minimal cognition perspective, we reframe this navigation as a subjective, information-driven sensorimotor process. To this end, we propose a framework linking a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) with biochemical reaction dynamics. Environmental variables are hidden, while the cell updates a minimal internal state from each observation through a memoryless Bayesian step. These internal dynamics balance orienting toward light with exploratory reorientation and can be implemented through Chemical-Reaction-Network Ordinary Differential Equations (CRN--ODEs). Our model includes a biophysical observation process for photoreception and a chemically computable polynomial bound on information gain. Using Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) on 30 experimentally recorded Chlamydomonas trajectories, we infer the behavioral objective consistent with observed phototactic motion and benchmark the resulting dynamics with standard Stochastic Simulation Algorithm (SSA) baselines. Our model reproduces the empirical alignment-to-light distribution, comparable to objective SSA baselines on this dataset. Within this framework, run--tumble alternation emerges as an information-acquisition strategy: tumbling reorients the cell to sample new sensory configurations and resolve sensor ambiguity, demonstrating how intracellular biochemical networks can support adaptive information-seeking behavior in cellular navigation.

</details>


### [162] [Neural Architecture Search for Generative Adversarial Networks: A Comprehensive Review and Critical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26169)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了神经架构搜索（NAS）在生成对抗网络（GANs）设计中的应用，分类比较了不同NAS方法（基于搜索策略、评估指标和性能结果），发现进化算法和梯度基方法在特定场景下表现优越，强调了超越传统IS和FID的稳健评估指标的重要性，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS已成为优化GANs设计的关键技术，能够自动化搜索有效架构并解决人工设计中的挑战，因此有必要对现有NAS-GAN方法进行系统梳理和比较。

Method: 采用综合性文献综述方法，按搜索策略、评估指标和性能结果等标准对现有NAS-GAN方法进行分类和比较分析。

Result: 进化算法和梯度基方法在特定上下文中表现出优越性；传统IS和FID评分不足以全面评估GAN性能，需要更稳健的评估指标；不同数据集的多样性对GAN性能评估至关重要。

Conclusion: 本文通过结构化比较现有NAS-GAN技术，旨在指导研究者开发更有效的NAS方法，推动GANs领域的发展。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has emerged as a pivotal technique in optimizing the design of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), automating the search for effective architectures while addressing the challenges inherent in manual design. This paper provides a comprehensive review of NAS methods applied to GANs, categorizing and comparing various approaches based on criteria such as search strategies, evaluation metrics, and performance outcomes. The review highlights the benefits of NAS in improving GAN performance, stability, and efficiency, while also identifying limitations and areas for future research. Key findings include the superiority of evolutionary algorithms and gradient-based methods in certain contexts, the importance of robust evaluation metrics beyond traditional scores like Inception Score (IS) and Fréchet Inception Distance (FID), and the need for diverse datasets in assessing GAN performance. By presenting a structured comparison of existing NAS-GAN techniques, this paper aims to guide researchers in developing more effective NAS methods and advancing the field of GANs.

</details>


### [163] [KG-TRACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Mechanistic Grounding in Antimicrobial Resistance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26179)
*Naman Garg,Sarika Jain,Sourav Yadav,Bharat K. Bhargava,Ghanapriya Singh,Abhishek Srivastava,Parimal Kar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KG-TRACE是一个将WHO突变知识图谱作为结构化生物约束整合到神经基因组模型中的神经符号框架，在结核分枝杆菌耐药性预测中实现了高准确率（异烟肼AUROC 0.9760）和92.5%的符号覆盖率，并通过引入生物接地比（BGR）量化神经归因与生物学知识的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于WGS的AMR预测模型虽准确率高，但缺乏将神经归因锚定于已建立的生物学通路的机制，无法提供可验证的临床审计轨迹。

Method: 提出KG-TRACE框架，通过可学习的认知信任门（epistemic trust gate）动态融合基因组特征与RotatE-based KG嵌入，在神经证据与符号生物学知识之间进行加权平衡；并引入BGR指标量化数据集级别的神经归因与生物学知识的对齐程度。

Result: 在CRyPTIC M. tuberculosis数据集上，异烟肼耐药预测AUROC达0.9760，符号覆盖率达92.5%；能有效识别MDR共现伪影，并为'UNCERTAIN'案例发出实验室随访标记。

Conclusion: 神经符号接地（neuro-symbolic grounding）为临床提供了可验证的审计轨迹，弥合了预测准确性与临床信任之间的差距。

Abstract: While WGS-based AMR prediction has reached high accuracy, existing models lack a mechanism to ground neural attributions in established biological pathways. We present KG-TRACE, a novel neuro-symbolic framework that integrates the WHO mutation knowledge graph (KG) as a structured biological constraint on a neural genomic model. Unlike existing methods that learn statistical patterns in isolation, KG-TRACE fuses genomic features and RotatE-based KG embeddings through a learned epistemic trust gate, dynamically weighting neural evidence against symbolic biological knowledge.
  Evaluated on the CRyPTIC M. tuberculosis cohort, KG-TRACE achieves an AUROC of 0.9760 for isoniazid, achieving competitive accuracy while its primary value lies in symbolic grounding, not predictive uplift. More importantly, we introduce the Biological Grounding Ratio (BGR), a dataset-level metric that quantifies alignment between neural attributions and established biology. Our framework achieves a 92.5% symbolic coverage of isoniazid-resistant predictions and effectively identifies MDR co-occurrence artifacts by issuing laboratory follow-up flags for 'UNCERTAIN' cases. We demonstrate that neuro-symbolic grounding provides a verifiable audit trail for clinicians, bridging the gap between predictive accuracy and clinical trust.

</details>


### [164] [Necessary but Not Sufficient: Temperature Control and Reproducibility in LLM-as-Judge Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26185)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM评判器即使设置temperature=0也无法完全消除结果波动，安全评估中的单次运行判决可能将噪声误报为安全属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证"设置temperature=0可使评判器确定性输出"这一广泛假设，揭示其在真实安全评估代码库中的失效情况。

Method: 基于日本AISI的开源aisev代码库，跨两个API提供商、三个模型层级、五种采样配置，共执行690次API调用，比较未设置温度、temperature=0及强制贪婪解码(top_k=1)条件下的结果一致性。

Result: (1)未设置temperature时，提供商默认使用1.0，边界附近项目在20次运行中结果不一致率高达~50%；(2)设置temperature=0后，7个边界项目中仍有1-2个不可复现；(3)Claude Opus 4.7/4.8已弃用temperature参数，导致主要缓解措施无法应用于新模型。

Conclusion: 评估框架应将评判器不一致性作为与分数同等重要的健康指标，而非报告单次运行判决。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge ("grader") components are now standard in evaluation harnesses, including safety evaluations where a pass/fail verdict may gate downstream deployment decisions. A widespread assumption is that setting the grader's sampling temperature to 0 makes grading deterministic. We test this assumption against a real safety-evaluation codebase (Japan AISI's open-source aisev) and show it fails on two levels. First, the harness invokes its grader without setting temperature or seed; the underlying provider silently applies its default of 1.0, so items near the decision boundary flip pass/fail across identical runs (per-item disagreement up to ~50% over 20 runs). Second, pinning temperature=0 reduces but does not eliminate flips: across 690 API calls spanning two providers, three model tiers, and five sampling configurations, 1-2 of 7 borderline items remain non-reproducible even under forced greedy decoding (top_k=1). Claude Opus 4.7/4.8 has since deprecated temperature entirely, rendering the primary mitigation inapplicable to newer model generations. These findings expose a structural gap: evaluation harnesses that report single-run verdicts without variance or grader-disagreement metrics can present noise as a safety property. We release a reproduction harness (690 calls, 7 conditions) and recommend that harnesses treat grader disagreement as a first-class health metric alongside the scores themselves.

</details>


### [165] [Clue-Guided Money Laundering Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26189)
*Boyang Wang,Jianing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Clue2Group框架，从初始线索出发通过分析师交互逐步恢复洗钱团伙结构，解决了现有图异常检测方法与实际反洗钱调查流程不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法主要产生节点级风险告警，全局团伙发现方法被动搜索可疑团伙，均与真实AML调查中分析师从具体线索逐步扩展调查的实际流程不匹配。

Method: 提出Clue2Group框架：1) 构建紧凑的局部调查上下文以降低噪声并保留链式和循环式洗钱结构；2) 使用多语义局部时序GNN估计线索条件局部风险场；3) 整合风险、结构和先验模式证据以恢复连贯的洗钱团伙。

Result: 在两个大规模反洗钱基准数据集上验证了Clue2Group的有效性，提供了实用的线索驱动分析框架。

Conclusion: Clue2Group为图基AML研究与实际调查工作流之间的鸿沟提供了可行的桥接方案。

Abstract: Money Laundering Group Discovery (MLGD) aims to identify hidden criminal groups and recover their complete structures in large-scale financial networks. Existing graph anomaly detection methods mainly produce node-level risk alerts, while global group discovery methods passively search for suspicious groups over the whole network. Both are mismatched with real Anti-money-laundering (AML) investigations, where analysts usually start from a concrete clue and gradually expand the investigation to recover the responsible group. To address this gap, we propose Clue-Guided Group Discovery (CGGD), where a laundering group is progressively recovered from an initial clue set through analyst interaction. We further propose Clue2Group, a framework that first constructs a compact local investigation context to reduce noise and preserve chain-like and cycle-like laundering structures. It then estimates a clue-conditioned local risk field with a multi-semantic local-temporal GNN, and finally integrates risk, structural, and prior-pattern evidence to recover a coherent laundering group. Experiments on two large-scale AML benchmarks show that Clue2Group provides a practical clue-driven analysis framework for AML investigations, offering a feasible step toward bridging the gap between graph-based AML research and real investigation workflows.

</details>


### [166] [Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26192)
*Jialan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦哈希投影潜在因子模型(FHPLF)，通过二元梯度矩阵、投影汉明距离和隐私增强上传策略，在联邦学习框架下实现高效、准确且保护隐私的哈希学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈希学习需将个人数据上传至中心服务器，违反日益严格的数据安全法规；联邦学习虽能保护隐私但存在通信开销大、梯度传输带来隐私风险的问题；现有哈希方法在联邦学习中的二值编码表示能力有限，导致模型精度下降。

Method: (a) 用二元梯度类矩阵替代实值梯度矩阵，降低计算/存储/通信成本并增强隐私保护；(b) 采用投影汉明距离进行相似性建模，捕获单个二值位的重要性以提升表示能力；(c) 提出安全二元梯度重组与隐私增强上传(SBG-PEU)策略，降低传输过程中的用户交互泄露风险。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，FHPLF持续优于最先进的哈希学习和联邦学习方法，在准确性、效率和隐私保护之间实现了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: FHPLF通过二元梯度矩阵和投影汉明距离等创新，有效解决了联邦学习中二值编码表示能力不足的问题，在保护隐私的同时保持了高效性和准确性。

Abstract: Hash Learning (HL) is an efficient representation learning approach that maps real-valued data into compact binary representations. Traditional HL methods typically require users to upload personal data to a central server, which is incompatible with increasingly stringent data security regulations. Federated Learning (FL) provides a decentralized paradigm for learning globally optimal models without centralizing private data. However, most FL methods rely on transmitting large-scale real-valued gradient information, leading to high communication overhead and potential privacy risks. Integrating HL into FL is a promising solution. Nevertheless, existing HL methods suffer from limited representational capacity of binary codes, which may degrade model accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose a Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor (FHPLF) model. FHPLF introduces three key innovations: (a) replacing real-valued gradient matrices with binary gradient-like matrices, significantly reducing computation, storage, and communication costs while enhancing privacy protection; (b) leveraging Projected Hamming Distance for similarity modeling, which captures the importance of individual binary bits to improve representation capability; and (c) proposing a Secure Binary Gradient Reassembly and Privacy-Enhanced Upload (SBG-PEU) strategy to further reduce the risk of user interaction leakage during transmission. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that FHPLF consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HL and FL methods, achieving a favorable trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and privacy preservation.

</details>


### [167] [Statistical and Structural Approaches to Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26200)
*Antonio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现代机器学习系统已从孤立预测工具演变为复杂的社会技术架构，算法公平性领域早期缓解策略因依赖确定性点估计和将个体视为孤立实体而存在根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法公平性领域的现有方法存在两个根本局限：审计时依赖确定性点估计，以及将个体视为脱离结构性背景的孤立实体，这限制了其在复杂社会技术环境中的有效性。

Method: 识别并针对当代公平性范式的两个根本局限（确定性点估计依赖和个体孤立化处理）进行理论批判与分析。

Result: 指出现有公平性缓解策略基于脆弱的简化假设，在复杂社会技术环境中效果有限。

Conclusion: 当代算法公平性范式需要超越确定性点估计和个体孤立化假设，以更有效地应对社会技术系统中的结构性不平等。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems have outgrown their origins as isolated predictive constructs, evolving into complex socio-technical architectures that actively mediate human opportunity. As algorithms increasingly determine access to economic and social opportunities, it has become widely recognized that these systems are deeply embedded with the structural inequalities and prejudices of their environments. The field of algorithmic fairness emerged in response to the growing recognition that models optimized for predictive accuracy can systematically disadvantage marginalized groups. Early mitigation strategies, however, rested on fragile simplifications that limited their effectiveness in complex socio-technical environments. This thesis identifies and addresses two fundamental limitations of contemporary fairness paradigms: the reliance on deterministic point estimates for auditing and the treatment of individuals as isolated entities devoid of structural context.

</details>


### [168] [Topology-Informed Neural Networks for Flood Detection in Optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26204)
*Sophia Li,Max Zhao,Raghu G. Raj,Tianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了拓扑数据分析（TDA）在洪水检测中的应用，通过在SEN12-FLOOD数据集上提取拓扑描述符并将其融入神经网络，证明拓扑特征既能独立携带洪水信号，又能补充现有网络，构建更鲁棒且可解释的洪水检测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习洪水检测方法（如ResNet-50+GRU、Vision Transformer）虽然性能优秀，但模型是黑箱，决策边界和内部推理难以解释。而洪水检测属于安全关键领域，需要可解释性。拓扑数据分析能捕捉数据的全局结构特征，适合分析具有几何可解释结构的洪水影像。

Method: 使用SEN12-FLOOD开放数据集，从每张影像中提取拓扑描述符（拓扑特征），将这些特征独立输入神经网络进行洪水检测，同时也将拓扑特征与现有网络（如CNN、Transformer）相结合，评估其对检测性能和可解释性的贡献。

Result: 拓扑描述符本身能携带有效的洪水信号，独立完成洪水检测；将其融入现有神经网络后，能够补充网络的特征表示，提升检测的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 拓扑数据分析为洪水检测提供了一种兼顾性能与可解释性的新范式，尤其在云层遮挡导致光学影像受限的场景下具有潜力，有望推动遥感灾害监测向更透明、可信的方向发展。

Abstract: Floods frequently impact regions around the world. Rapid and accurate flood detection is crucial for emergency response and timely mitigation of human and economic loss. The expanding availability of satellite data and advances in artificial intelligence have enhanced monitoring of environmental hazards, but many flood events remain challenging to detect because cloud cover obscures optical satellite imagery. Rambour et al. introduced the SEN12-FLOOD dataset and extracted per-image features using a ResNet-50 convolutional neural network backbone, then fed these features into a gated recurrent unit network to show that temporal information can substantially improve accuracy compared to single-image baselines. More recently, Chamatidis et al. showed that a vision transformer can achieve strong performance with popular convolutional architectures. However, these models typically function as opaque black boxes, making it difficult to interpret their decision boundaries, learned features, and internal reasoning, especially in safety-critical domains like remote sensing. In contrast, topological data analysis (TDA) provides a mathematically grounded framework for capturing global structural features of data. TDA has emerged as a powerful tool for analyzing complex imagery, especially imagery with geometrically interpretable structures, of which floods are a prime candidate. In this work, we systematically evaluate topological descriptors for flood detection using the open-source SEN12-FLOOD dataset. By extracting topological features from each image and incorporating them into neural networks, we demonstrate that topological descriptors carry meaningful flood signals independently and complement existing networks to yield more robust and interpretable flood detection systems.

</details>


### [169] [A General Framework for Learning Algebraic Properties from Cayley Graphs using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26212)
*Tal Weissblat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与具体属性无关的图神经网络框架，直接从Cayley图中学习有限群的代数性质（如阿贝尔性、幂零性、可解性），实验表明该框架能够成功从图表示中区分多种代数性质，证明图表示中编码了丰富的代数信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作[1]提出了一个用于预测有限群可解性的GNN框架，本文旨在将该方法推广为与具体代数性质无关的一般性框架，探索能否直接从Cayley图表示中恢复多种代数结构信息。

Method: 采用统一的GNN架构和训练流程，以有限群的Cayley图作为输入，分别学习阿贝尔性、幂零性和可解性这三种代表性代数性质，从多个群族中采集数据集进行训练和评估。

Result: 在来自多个群族的有限群集合上，该框架成功学习并区分了多种代数性质，表明GNN能够从Cayley图中提取有效的代数结构信息。

Conclusion: Cayley图表示中编码了丰富的代数信息，GNN能够有效提取这些信息；该工作为将图表示学习应用于有限群代数性质研究提供了概念验证。

Abstract: A Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework for predicting the solvability of finite groups from their Cayley graph representations was introduced in [1]. In the present work, we generalize this approach and develop a property-independent framework for learning algebraic properties of finite groups directly from Cayley graphs. As representative case studies, we consider abelianity, nilpotency, and solvability. Using a common GNN architecture and training pipeline, we investigate the extent to which algebraic structure can be recovered from graph-based representations alone. Results on a collection of finite groups drawn from several families demonstrate that the framework successfully learns and distinguishes multiple algebraic properties from their associated Cayley graphs. These findings suggest that substantial algebraic information is encoded in graph representations and can be extracted through GNNs. More broadly, the proposed framework provides a proof of concept for applying graph representation learning to the study of algebraic properties of finite groups.

</details>


### [170] [Fast LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26217)
*Yuntian Gao,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fast-LeWM通过动作前缀并行预测替代LeWM的自回归逐一步态预测，在提升规划成功率的同时大幅降低规划时间并减缓潜在误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWM等JEPA架构在视觉规划中需重复进行单步隐状态转换的自回归展开，导致计算成本高且随规划视界增长累积潜在误差。

Method: 提出Fast-LeWM，以动作前缀为基本预测单元，并行编码候选动作序列的各前缀并直接预测执行这些前缀后的未来隐状态，替代逐一步态滚动。

Result: 在多任务上Fast-LeWM相比LeWM提升了平均成功率，显著降低了规划时间，并实现了更低的开环潜在损失，且损失增长随规划视界延长明显变慢。

Conclusion: 动作前缀级监督使模型学习状态在不同动作前缀下的连续演化，而非仅拟合单步状态转移，从而在规划时无需逐帧遍历中间想象状态即可评估未来隐状态。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), including recent LeWorldModel (LeWM), have become a promising foundation for reconstruction-free visual world models. For visual planning, however, LeWM evaluates candidate action sequences by repeatedly applying a local one-step latent transition model. This autoregressive rollout makes planning computationally expensive and exposes the predicted trajectory to accumulated latent errors as the horizon grows. We propose Fast LeWorldModel (Fast-LeWM), a fast latent world model that replaces repeated local rollout with action-prefix prediction. Given the current latent and a candidate action sequence, Fast-LeWM encodes its prefixes and predicts the future latents reached after executing those prefixes in parallel. By making action prefixes the basic prediction unit, Fast-LeWM directly models action effects accumulated to different extents over multiple horizons. This prefix-level supervision forces the model to learn how states continuously evolve under different action prefixes, rather than only fitting one-step state transitions. During planning, the predictor can use the last prefix token from the encoded action sequence to evaluate the corresponding future latent without explicitly rolling through each intermediate imagined state. Across multiple tasks, Fast-LeWM improves average success over LeWM while substantially reducing planning time, achieving lower open-loop latent loss whose growth becomes significantly slower as the rollout horizon increases.

</details>


### [171] [Dataset Usage Inference without Shadow Models or Held-out Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26257)
*Wojciech Łapacz,Stanisław Pawlak,Jan Dubiński,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的数据集使用推断（DUI）框架，无需训练影子模型或使用真实保留数据，通过生成合成非成员样本、提取多样化的成员信号并将DUI视为混合比例估计问题，来可靠地量化大型图像生成模型中数据集的使用比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DUI方法依赖于不切实际的假设：需要训练昂贵的影子模型来模仿目标模型，并且需要同时访问已知的训练样本和确认未参与训练的在分布保留集。这些条件使得现有方法无法应用于现代大型模型和真实数据所有权纠纷场景。

Method: 1) 生成合成非成员样本替代真实保留数据；2) 提取多样化的成员信号；3) 将DUI转化为混合比例估计问题，估算候选数据集中被用于训练的比例。

Result: 在大规模图像生成模型上的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地量化数据集使用比例。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据所有者提供了一种实用工具，用于判断自身数据有多少被用于模型训练，解决了现有DUI方法在实践中的不可行性问题。

Abstract: How much of my data was used to train a machine learning model? Dataset Usage Inference (DUI) aims to answer this by estimating what fraction of a dataset contributed to a model's training. However, existing DUI methods rely on assumptions that rarely hold in practice: they require training expensive shadow models to imitate the target model, and they assume access to both known training samples and an in-distribution held-out set confirmed to be absent from training. These conditions make current approaches impractical for modern large models and real data ownership disputes. We introduce a practical DUI framework that removes these constraints. Our method requires neither shadow models nor real held-out data. Instead, it generates synthetic non-member samples, extracts diverse membership signals, and casts DUI as a mixture proportion estimation problem to estimate what share of the candidate dataset was used during training. Experiments on large image generative models show that our method reliably quantifies dataset usage, providing a practical tool for data owners to determine how much of their data was used to train a model.

</details>


### [172] [Zero-Shot Size Transfer for Neural ODEs on Sparse Random Graphs: Graphon Limits and Adjoint Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26662)
*Mingsong Yan,Zhida Wang,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经微分方程（GNDE）的零样本尺寸迁移原理，建立了图ON极限理论，证明了GNDE解向图ON-NDE解的收敛速率，以及离散-优化（DTO）与优化-离散（OTD）训练的一致性，并通过实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNDE的局部、尺寸无关的滤波器暗示了零样本尺寸迁移的可能性（从小图训练部署到大图），但缺乏定量理论支持。本文旨在建立GNDE在稀疏随机图上的收敛理论与迁移学习的理论保证。

Method: 将图ON神经微分方程（Graphon-NDE）和对偶Graphon-NDE定义为前向和对偶GNDE系统的无限节点极限，并证明适定性。对于n节点稀疏随机图（稀疏参数α_n），证明GNDE解以O((α_n n)^{-1/2})速率（对数因子内）高概率收敛到Graphon-NDE解。研究显式欧拉离散化下DTO与OTD训练，证明两者渐近一致性，状态和局部参数梯度差异分别为O(1/M)和O(1/M^2)。

Result: 建立了GNDE在图ON极限下的收敛理论；证明了DTO与OTD训练的一致性；实验在HSBM和tent图ON上验证了理论速率，在四类图ON上的零样本迁移实验展示了学习到的GNDE在更大独立采样图上的准确部署。

Conclusion: GNDE具有零样本尺寸迁移的理论保证，图ON极限为理解GNDE在稀疏随机图上的行为提供了严格的数学框架，DTO与OTD训练在高分辨率离散化下等价。

Abstract: Graph Neural Differential Equations (GNDEs) model continuous-time graph dynamics by parameterizing Neural ODE velocity fields with Graph Neural Networks. Their local, size-independent filters suggest a zero-shot size-transfer principle: train on a small graph and deploy on larger, similar graphs without retraining. We develop a quantitative theory for this principle on sparse random graphs sampled from graphons. We consider Graphon Neural Differential Equations (Graphon-NDEs) and adjoint Graphon-NDEs as the infinite-node limits of the forward and adjoint GNDE systems, and establish well-posedness. For an $n$-node random graph with sparsity parameter $α_n$, we prove trajectory-wise convergence of GNDE solutions to Graphon-NDE solutions at rate $O((α_n n)^{-1/2})$, up to logarithmic factors, with high probability. We also establish uniform-in-time convergence bounds for adjoint systems governing hidden-state and parameter gradients. We further study discretize-then-optimize (DTO) and optimize-then-discretize (OTD) training. Under explicit Euler discretization with $M$ steps, we show that DTO and OTD are asymptotically consistent, with hidden-state and local parameter-gradient discrepancies of orders $O(1/M)$ and $O(1/M^2)$, respectively, up to sparsity and logarithmic factors. Experiments on HSBM and tent graphons support the theoretical rates, while zero-shot transfer experiments across four graphon classes demonstrate accurate deployment of learned GNDEs on larger independently sampled graphs.

</details>


### [173] [Equivariance and Augmentation for Bayesian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26273)
*Miaowen Dong,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）中数据增强与等变性的关系，推导了变分推理下达到精确等变的条件，提出了三种新的对称化技术，其中轨道扩展方法在等变性和整体性能上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性在深度学习中至关重要，但关于是使用等变网络还是数据增强存在争议。以往数据增强的理论分析很少，本文旨在通过贝叶斯神经网络和变分推理来理论分析数据增强能否达到精确等变性。

Method: 1) 聚焦指数族变分分布的BNN，推导数据增强达到精确等变的条件；2) 推导等变误差的界限；3) 提出三种新型对称化技术（包括轨道扩展）来增强数据增强效果；4) 进行数值实验验证。

Result: 推导出了指数族变分分布下达到精确等变性的条件，获得了等变误差的界限，所提出的轨道扩展对称化方法在等变性和整体性能上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 数据增强在贝叶斯神经网络变分推理中可以实现精确等变性，且通过合适的对称化设计（如轨道扩展）能够进一步提升性能，为理解对称性在深度学习中的应用提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Symmetries are important for many deep learning tasks, ranging from applications in the sciences to medical imaging. However, there is an ongoing debate about whether to impose symmetry constraints on the neural network architecture (yielding equivariant neural networks) or learn them from augmented training data. Although equivariant networks are well-studied theoretically, much less is known about data augmentation, since analyzing augmentation requires control over the training dynamics. Inspired by recent results that show that augmented infinite deep ensembles are exactly equivariant, we study data augmentation for Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) trained with variational inference. We focus on variational distributions in the exponential family and derive conditions under which exact equivariance is reached. We furthermore obtain bounds on the equivariance error and introduce three novel symmetrization techniques which boost the effect of data augmentation in this setting. We conduct extensive numerical experiments which show that one of our symmetrization methods (orbit expansion) outperforms the baseline in both equivariance and overall performance. Our code is available at github.com/dmw1998/augment-BNNs

</details>


### [174] [Finding Stationary Points by Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27082)
*Helin Wang,Chenyi Zhang,Xiwen Tao,Yexin Zhang,Tongyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅通过比较 oracle（比较两个点的函数值大小）访问非凸函数时，寻找 ε-驻点的问题。针对具有 Lipschitz 梯度和 Hessian 的二阶可微函数，提出了经典算法（~O(n²/ε¹·⁵) 次查询）和量子算法（~O(n/ε¹·⁵) 次查询）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际问题中无法直接获得函数梯度或完整函数值，只能通过两两比较得到函数值大小关系（如 A/B 测试、黑箱优化等）。如何在仅依赖这种弱监督信号（比较 oracle）的情况下，高效找到非凸函数的驻点是一个重要且具挑战性的问题。

Method: 经典算法：设计了一个子程序，用 ~O(n² log(1/δ)) 次查询估计归一化 Hessian 到精度 δ，然后基于该估计构建优化算法。量子算法：在量子比较 oracle 模型下（支持叠加态查询），利用量子优势将查询复杂度降至 ~O(n/ε¹·⁵)。

Result: 经典算法查询复杂度为 ~O(n²/ε¹·⁵)，量子算法查询复杂度为 ~O(n/ε¹·⁵)，均达到 ε-驻点。

Conclusion: 在仅依赖比较 oracle 的非凸优化问题中，首次实现了对 Hessian 的显式估计并用于加速优化；量子算法展示了平方级的维度加速，为量子优化在弱监督场景下的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study the problem of finding stationary points of non-convex functions when access to the objective is provided only through a comparison oracle that, given two points, outputs which has the larger function value. For a twice differentiable $f\colon\mathbb R^n\to\mathbb R$ with Lipschitz gradient and Hessian, we develop an algorithm that visits an $ε$-stationary point using $\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$ queries. Our approach uses a subroutine that estimates the normalized Hessian to accuracy $δ$ using $\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$ queries. We further study this problem with a quantum comparison oracle model where queries can be made in superpositions, and develop the first quantum algorithm that finds an $ε$-stationary point, which takes $\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$ queries.

</details>


### [175] [SSM Adapters via Hankel Reduced-order Modeling: Injection Site Determines Task Suitability in Long-Context Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26290)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HRM适配器，一种基于状态空间模型的参数高效微调方法，通过Hankel平衡截断初始化，在长上下文任务中优于LoRA，实现计算复杂度与LoRA持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索参数高效微调在需要序列状态累积的任务中，能否从SSM适配器中获益，以及MLP模块是否是更好的注入位置。

Method: 提出基于状态空间模型的HRM适配器，利用经验Hankel Gramians的平衡截断进行初始化，利用系统矩阵的时间不变性实现基于FFT的并行扫描，达到与LoRA相同的计算复杂度。

Result: 在Mistral-7B上（8.4M可训练参数），HRM在LongBench任务中优于LoRA变体，QuALITY相对准确率提升34.8%，QMSum相对ROUGE-1提升71.6%，在18种合成配置中表现一致优越。

Conclusion: HRM适配器能有效学习调节循环行为，为长上下文序列建模提供了一种优于低秩适应的鲁棒架构替代方案。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) typically targets attention projectors, its efficacy for tasks requiring sequential state accumulation remains under-explored. We examine if PEFT for such tasks can benefit from state space model (SSMs) adapters, and if MLP blocks are better injection sites. We introduce Hankel Reduced order Model (HRM) adapter, an SSM-based residual module initialized via Balanced Truncation of empirical Hankel Grammians. By leveraging the time-invariance of the system matrix $\bar{A}$, HRM enables an exact FFT-based parallel scan, achieving computational parity with LoRA across all context lengths. In iso-parametric evaluations on Mistral-7B (8.4M trainable parameters), HRM outperforms LoRA variants on LongBench tasks, including QuALITY (+34.8\% relative accuracy) and QMSum (+71.6\% relative ROUGE-1). HRM further demonstrates consistent superiority across 18 configurations of synthetic state-tracking (DFA, Parity) and character-level language modeling (enwik8). Gate analysis reveals that HRM adapters effectively learn to modulate recurrence, providing a robust architectural alternative to low-rank adaptation for long-context sequence modeling.

</details>


### [176] [The Red Queen Gödel Machine: Co-Evolving Agents and Their Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26294)
*Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanović,William F. Shen,Daniel Burkhardt,Meghdad Kurmanji,Nurbek Tastan,Lorenzo Sani,Niccolò Alberto Elia Venanzi,Ambroise Odonnat,Zeyu Cao,Bill Marino,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出红皇后哥德尔机（RQGM），一种用于非平稳效用下递归自我改进的进化框架。RQGM通过将搜索组织为固定评估标准的epoch，并允许在epoch边界更新效用，实现了进化评估器、对抗目标和动态效用的自我改进。实验表明，RQGM在可验证编码任务上以更少token超越SOTA，在科学论文写作/评审和奥赛级证明写作/评分中显著提升性能，并能纠正基线评审者对AI生成论文的过度接受问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进智能体的搜索方法假设评估标准是平稳不变的（固定验证器、基准或标注数据集），忽略了进化中物种与环境的共变原理。作者旨在将评估纳入改进循环，打开搜索空间以适应进化评估器、对抗目标和动态效用，从而超越静态基准。

Method: 提出红皇后哥德尔机（RQGM），通过将搜索组织为epoch来实现受控效用进化：每个epoch内评估标准固定，epoch边界处可更新效用。这使得自我改进保证在每个epoch内成立，同时目标可在epoch间演化。方法结合了agent-as-a-judge代码评审信号、共进化写作/评审和对抗目标。

Result: 1) 编码任务：RQGM在可验证编码任务上超越SOTA，使用1.35x-1.72x更少token。2) 论文写作：共进化写作者在多样化评审面板下达到1.78x-1.86x更高的接受率。3) 论文评审：共进化评审员达到9%更高的真实准确率，且RQGM通过对抗目标纠正了基线评审者以1.91x人类速率过度接受AI生成论文的问题。4) 奥赛级证明：在证明写作和评分上均显著提升。

Conclusion: RQGM成功将进化原理引入递归自我改进，通过受控效用演化实现了超越静态基准的性能。实验证明该框架在编码、科学写作和数学证明等多个领域有效，并能解决AI评估中的系统性偏差问题，为构建更稳健的自我改进智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Self-improving agents are state-of-the-art (SOTA) on agentic coding benchmarks and have recently been extended to general domains. However, their search methods generally assume a stationary evaluation criterion: a fixed verifier, benchmark, or labeled dataset that remains valid as the agent improves. This ignores a central feature of evolution: species adapt as their environments change with them. We aim to bring the same principle to recursive self-improvement, making evaluation part of the improvement loop and opening search to evolving evaluators, adversarial objectives, and dynamic utilities that may surpass static benchmarks. We introduce the Red Queen Godel Machine (RQGM), an evolutionary framework for recursive self-improvement under non-stationary utilities. The RQGM makes this possible through controlled utility evolution: search is organized into epochs with a fixed within-epoch evaluation criterion, while the utility can be updated at epoch boundaries, so self-improvement guarantees hold per epoch as the objective evolves across them. We begin by showing that even on verifiable coding tasks, the RQGM improves test pass rate over the prior SOTA by adding a complementary agent-as-a-judge code-review signal. This signal is cheaper and the RQGM uses 1.35x-1.72x fewer tokens. We then turn to scientific paper writing and reviewing, and Olympiad-level proof writing and grading, where the RQGM improves performance over prior self-improving agents: co-evolved writers reach 1.78x-1.86x higher acceptance rates under a diverse agent-as-a-judge panel, while co-evolved graders reach 9% higher ground-truth accuracy. In paper reviewing, the strongest baseline reviewer over-accepts AI-generated papers at up to 1.91x the human rate. The RQGM corrects this by introducing an adversarial objective that discovers reviewers equally stringent on AI and human work.

</details>


### [177] [High-Probability PL-SGD with Markovian Noise: Optimal Mixing and Tail Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26316)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Aprameyo Chakrabartty,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件下，梯度样本由外生马尔可夫链生成的一阶优化方法。针对轻尾情形，通过滞后期分组论证，将SGD的高概率界从$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$改进至最优的$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$；针对重尾情形，设计了全样本截断分组方法，实现了$\widetilde{O}(\sigma_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$的高概率随机误差，并建立了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于马尔可夫梯度采样下SGD的均匀高概率界存在一个间隙：期望界为$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$，但高概率界仅为$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$。此外，重尾马尔可夫梯度场景下的高概率分析尚缺乏系统的理论刻画。

Method: 对于轻尾情形，提出滞后期分组(lag-blocking)论证方法，建立均匀高概率保证，主随机项为$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/(k+K_0))$，并通过两状态持久链的二次目标证明下界$\Omega(\sigma^2 t_{mix}/k)$。对于重尾情形($p\in(1,2]$)，设计全样本截断分组(all-samples clipped block)方法，利用每步马尔可夫转移同时减轻马尔可夫偏差，并通过粘性马尔可夫链的重尾均值估计建立匹配下界。

Result: 轻尾情形：将高概率界从$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$优化至$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$，并证明该线性依赖关系是最优的。重尾情形：在转移预算$T$下，高概率随机误差为$\widetilde{O}(\sigma_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$，同样匹配下界。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了轻尾PL-SGD中混合时间的最优多项式依赖关系，以及在稳健机制下重尾指数和有效样本量的最优依赖关系，填补了马尔可夫梯度采样下SGD高概率分析的理论空白。

Abstract: We study first-order methods for smooth objectives satisfying the Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PL) condition when gradient samples are generated by an exogenous Markov chain. In the light-tailed setting, prior uniform-in-time high-probability bounds for ordinary Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) under a standard growth envelope scale as $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$, leaving a gap with the $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$ expectation bounds. We close this gap using a lag-blocking argument to establish a uniform high-probability guarantee with a leading stochastic term of $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/(k+K_0))$ under geometric mixing. We prove this linear dependence on the mixing time is optimal via a matching $Ω(σ^2 t_{mix}/k)$ lower bound on a quadratic objective driven by a persistent two-state chain.
  We then extend this framework to heavy-tailed Markovian gradients satisfying a stationary finite-$p$-moment condition, $p \in (1,2]$. We design an all-samples clipped block method that uses every Markov transition while mitigating Markovian bias. Under a transition budget $T$, this algorithm achieves a high-probability stochastic error of $\widetilde{O}(σ_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$. We establish a matching lower bound by reducing PL optimization to heavy-tailed mean estimation for a sticky Markov chain. Ultimately, this work tightly characterizes the optimal polynomial dependence on mixing time for light-tailed PL-SGD, and the optimal heavy-tail exponent and effective-sample-size dependence in the robust regime.

</details>


### [178] [EVOM: Agentic Meta-Evolution of Actor-Critic Architectures for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26327)
*Boyun Zhang,Chao Wang,Kai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVOM提出了一种基于大语言模型设计智能体的元进化框架，用于自动发现高性能的Actor-Critic架构。该方法通过双层优化（内层PPO训练权重，外层元进化迭代优化架构程序）并结合LLM设计器，在强化学习任务中超越了手动设计基线和现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Actor-Critic强化学习中，网络架构通常需要手动设计，而自动化设计面临两大挑战：每个候选架构必须经过训练才能评估，且设计空间是开放式的。为克服这些困难，作者提出了EVOM框架。

Method: 将架构搜索建模为双层优化：内层通过低保真度的PPO算法训练权重；外层通过基于LLM的设计智能体迭代精炼架构程序，从而驱动元进化。该LLM设计智能体纯粹作为架构设计者，与策略执行和环境控制完全解耦。

Result: 在Ant-v4和HalfCheetah-v4环境中，EVOM优于手动设计基线、LLM引导的随机搜索以及最先进的LLM引导程序化策略搜索方法MLES。消融实验验证了元进化循环和LLM设计智能体对最终性能都不可或缺。

Conclusion: EVOM通过元进化与LLM设计智能体的结合，有效实现了Actor-Critic架构的自动化搜索，为强化学习中的神经架构设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: In actor-critic reinforcement learning, network architectures are typically manually designed. Automating this design is challenging because each candidate must be trained before evaluation, and the design space is open-ended. To address these challenges, we introduce EVOM, an agentic meta-evolution framework for discovering high-performance actor-critic architectures. We frame architecture search as a bi-level optimization: an inner loop trains weights via the low-fidelity proximal policy optimization (PPO), while an outer loop drives meta-evolution by iteratively refining architecture programs. Crucially, this outer loop is powered by an LLM-based design agent that operates purely as an architecture designer, completely decoupled from policy execution and environment control. Experiments reveal that EVOM outperforms the manually designed baseline, an LLM-guided random search, and the state-of-the-art LLM-guided programmatic policy search method MLES, delivering superior performance on Ant-v4 and HalfCheetah-v4. Ablation studies validate that both the meta-evolution loop and the LLM Design Agent are indispensable for final performance.

</details>


### [179] [Mesh-RL: Coupled subgrid reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26333)
*Behnam Gheshlaghi,Bahador Rashidi,Shahin Atakishiyev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mesh-RL，一种受有限元方法和区域分解理论启发的空间域分解框架，通过将环境划分为重叠子网格并强制执行边界一致的时序差分更新，实现局部学习与全局值传播的协调，从而加速稀疏奖励环境中的长程信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模或稀疏奖励环境中，强化学习的时序差分奖励传播速度缓慢，值信息仅能在状态空间中局部传播，导致长程信用分配效率低下。

Method: Mesh-RL将环境划分为重叠子网格，并强制执行边界一致的时序差分更新。该方法不修改奖励函数、Bellman算子或引入显式规划机制，而是通过空间域分解实现局部学习与全局值传播的协调。

Result: 在危险密集的网格世界环境中，Mesh-RL在Q-learning、SARSA和Dyna-Q上均持续提升了收敛速度、累积奖励和学习稳定性。更高网格分辨率可维持探索、防止过早收敛并加速值传播。Dyna-Q在结构化分解下仍能获得额外增益。

Conclusion: Mesh-RL提供了一种 principled 的空间域分解机制来加速时序差分学习，将科学计算中的有限元边界一致技术桥接到强化学习中，提升了稀疏奖励环境的样本效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in large or sparse-reward environments suffers from slow temporal-difference reward propagation, as value information spreads only locally across the state space. We propose Mesh-RL, a spatial domain-decomposition framework inspired by the finite element method and domain decomposition theory, which partitions the environment into overlapping subgrids and enforces boundary-consistent temporal-difference updates. Such an approach enables localized learning while ensuring globally coherent value propagation. Unlike hierarchical or model-based approaches, Mesh-RL accelerates long-range credit assignment without modifying the reward function, Bellman operator, or introducing explicit planning mechanisms. We evaluate Mesh-RL on hazard-dense grid-world environments with varying geometries and mesh resolutions. Across Q-learning, SARSA, and Dyna-Q, Mesh-RL consistently improves convergence speed, cumulative reward, and learning stability. Higher mesh resolutions sustain exploration, prevent premature convergence, and substantially accelerate value propagation to distant states. While Dyna-Q already benefits from internal planning, it still achieves additional gains under structured decomposition. Overall, Mesh-RL introduces a principled spatial domain-decomposition mechanism for accelerating temporal-difference learning. Our framework bridges finite element method-inspired boundary-consistency techniques from scientific computing with reinforcement learning to improve sample efficiency in sparse-reward environments. We will release source code of the study.

</details>


### [180] [EMA-FS: Accelerating GBDT Training via Gain-Informed Feature Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26337)
*Yan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于EMA的特征筛选方法EMA-FS及其随机变体S-EMA-FS，通过维护特征分裂增益的指数移动平均来动态选择Top-K特征构建直方图，在保持与LightGBM完全兼容的同时实现了最高2.61倍的训练加速，且在稠密中高维数据上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBDT（如LightGBM）在训练时65-70%的时间用于构建每个特征的直方图，现有方法如随机特征子采样（feature_fraction）不考虑特征的预测效用，导致信息浪费。需要一种算法级优化来在保留有效特征的同时减少不必要的直方图构建开销。

Method: EMA-FS维护每个特征在提升迭代中分裂增益的指数移动平均，经过短暂预热后只对历史增益排名Top-K的特征构建直方图；S-EMA-FS用增益加权随机采样替代确定性Top-K选择，通过集中参数beta统一了确定性筛选（beta→∞）和随机子采样（beta=0）。两种方法均在LightGBM六个树学习器中以约120行C++实现。

Result: 在500维合成基准上实现2.61倍加速，在432维IEEE-CIS Fraud数据集上30%保留率下提速1.45倍；70%保留率下AUC提升0.11点同时实现1.34倍加速。在极端稀疏数据（Bosch，>90%缺失）上无加速，因为LightGBM的稀疏分箱优化已绕过空值。

Conclusion: EMA-FS是一种有效的、有信息的特征筛选方法，能在不改变核心例程的前提下显著加速稠密中高维数据的GBDT训练，且S-EMA-FS提供了从确定性筛选到随机子采样的统一框架。

Abstract: Gradient Boosted Decision Trees (GBDT), exemplified by LightGBM, spend a dominant fraction of training time -- typically 65-70% -- constructing per-feature histograms. Existing approaches such as random feature subsampling (feature_fraction) discard features without regard for their predictive utility.
