We propose EMA-based Feature Screening (EMA-FS), an algorithm-level optimization that maintains an exponential moving average (EMA) of per-feature split gains across boosting iterations and, after a short warmup, restricts histogram construction to the top-K features ranked by historical gain. Unlike random subsampling, EMA-FS is informed: it retains high-gain features while screening out low-gain ones. Operating at the per-tree level, it preserves full compatibility with LightGBM's histogram subtraction trick, requiring no changes to core routines.
  We evaluate EMA-FS on datasets spanning financial fraud detection, advertising click-through prediction, industrial quality control, and synthetic benchmarks, with feature dimensionalities from 29 to 968. On dense, moderate-to-high-dimensional data it achieves significant speedups: 2.61x on a 500-feature synthetic benchmark and 1.45x on the 432-feature IEEE-CIS Fraud dataset at 30% retention. At 70% retention it improves AUC by 0.11 points while delivering a 1.34x speedup. On extremely sparse data (Bosch, >90% missing) it yields no speedup, as LightGBM's sparse bin optimization already bypasses empty values.
  We further introduce Stochastic EMA-FS (S-EMA-FS), which replaces deterministic top-K selection with gain-weighted random sampling controlled by a concentration parameter beta, unifying deterministic EMA-FS (beta -> infinity) and random subsampling (beta = 0) in one framework. Both are implemented in ~120 lines of C++ across all six LightGBM tree learners and are fully backward-compatible.

</details>


### [181] [Does Aurora Encode Atmospheric Structure? Latent Regime Analysis and Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26361)
*Emma Kasteleyn,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过空间池化PCA和层相关性传播（LRP）分析了Aurora基础模型的内部表征，发现其潜空间主要按季节周期组织，极端风暴事件未形成线性可分离聚类；LRP显示模型关注与1987年大风暴三维垂直结构一致的特征，扰动测试中遮蔽相关区域导致预报误差比随机遮蔽高3.31倍，表明Aurora无需显式指令即可学习气象一致性和垂直结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML基础模型虽能高效模拟大气动力学，但作为不透明的"黑箱"运作，缺乏可解释性。本文旨在探究Aurora模型内部表征的物理意义和组织结构。

Method: 采用空间池化PCA分析潜空间组织结构，使用层相关性传播（LRP）进行归因分析，并设计扰动测试验证关键区域对预报的重要性。

Result: 潜空间主要按季节周期组织，极端风暴事件未形成独立聚类；LRP识别出模型关注与风暴三维垂直结构一致的特征；扰动相关区域导致预报误差3.31倍于随机遮蔽。

Conclusion: Aurora模型在无显式物理约束条件下，自发学习了具有气象学一致性的表征，包括季节循环和垂直结构，为提升气象AI模型的可解释性提供了重要依据。

Abstract: ML foundation models are able to emulate atmospheric dynamics accurately and efficiently but operate as opaque ``black boxes''. We investigate the internal representations of the Aurora model using spatially pooled PCA and layer-wise relevance propagation (LRP). We find evidence that Aurora's latent space is primarily organized by seasonal cycles, whereas extreme storm events do not form a linearly separable cluster. LRP indicates that the model attends to features consistent with the 3D vertical structure of the Great Storm of 1987. Perturbation tests show masking relevant regions degrades forecasts $3.31\times$ more than random masking. These findings suggest that Aurora learns meteorological coherence and vertical structure without explicit instruction.

</details>


### [182] [SOLAR: AI-Powered Speed-of-Light Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26383)
*Qijing Huang,Sana Damani,Zhifan Ye,Athinagoras Skiadopoulos,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Jason Clemons,Sahil Modi,Jingquan Wang,Aditya Kane,Edward C Lin,Humphrey Shi,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLAR是一个自动从PyTorch和JAX源码推导并验证理论最小执行时间（Speed-of-Light bounds）的框架，结合LLM前端和确定性分析流程，支持多保真度分析，在KernelBench、JAX/Flax模型和机器人任务中展示了四项优化应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Speed-of-Light（SOL）分析需手动推导，易出错且无法跟上快速模型开发节奏，缺乏自动化框架来回答"深度学习模型在目标硬件上能跑多快"以及"当前实现距离理论极限有多远"这两个核心问题。

Method: SOLAR采用混合流程：(1) LLM前端将PyTorch/JAX程序翻译为可执行的Affine Loop IR，并通过输出对比验证；(2) 确定性流程将IR提升为einsum图；(3) 分析后端计算未融合、融合及缓存感知的SOL边界。框架支持多保真度分析，覆盖多种算子与语言。

Result: SOLAR在KernelBench、JAX/Flax模型和机器人任务上验证了其有效性，实现了零SOL违规的边界验证，并能通过多保真度分析收紧边界、揭示优化洞察。展示了四项用例：多级别余量分析、识别优化机会、跨平台探索以及逆向Roofline硬件配置。

Conclusion: SOLAR证明了自动化的SOL分析是可行且实用的，为深度学习模型在目标硬件上的性能极限分析提供了系统化工具，能够有效桥接触发器开发与硬件优化之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: How fast could a deep-learning model run on target hardware, and how far is today's implementation from that limit? These questions are central to software, hardware, and algorithm optimizations. Speed-of-Light (SOL) analysis answers them by computing a workload's theoretical minimum execution time on a given architecture. Yet deriving SOL bounds remains manual, error-prone, and disconnected from rapid model development. To close this gap, we introduce SOLAR, a framework that automatically derives validated SOL bounds from PyTorch and JAX source code. SOLAR leverages both generative and deterministic components in its flow: an LLM frontend translates any source programs into an executable Affine Loop IR, validated by output comparison; a deterministic flow lifts the IR into an einsum graph; and an analytical backend computes unfused, fused, and cache-aware SOL bounds. SOLAR provides comprehensive operator and language coverage, produces validated bounds with zero observed SOL violations, and offers multi-fidelity analysis that tightens bounds and surfaces optimization insights. We evaluate SOLAR across KernelBench, JAX/Flax models, and robotics workloads. These experiments demonstrate four use cases: headroom analysis at multiple fidelity levels, identifying optimization opportunities, cross-platform exploration, and inverse-roofline hardware provisioning.

</details>


### [183] [At the Edge of Understanding: Sparse Autoencoders Trace The Limits of Transformer Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26396)
*Praneet Suresh,Jack Stanley,Sonia Joseph,Luca Scimeca,Danilo Bzdok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机制框架，用于刻画Transformer模型的鲁棒性边界。研究发现，OOD输入（包括拼写错误和越狱提示）会导致语言模型内部产生更多错误概念。基于此，作者开发了一种量化分布偏移的方法，并提出了一种基于机制的微调策略来增强LLM的鲁棒性。同时，将OOD概念从输入数据扩展至模型的内部计算过程，为推理时的Transformer诊断提供了新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中模型常面临与训练数据分布不同的意外或对抗性数据，而Transformer缺乏明确的机制处理此类偏移，导致可靠性和安全性下降。因此，需要对Transformer在OOD场景下的表现进行更严谨的研究。

Method: 通过系统实验，提出一个基于机制的框架来刻画Transformer模型的鲁棒性轮廓。发现OOD输入会驱动语言模型在内部使用更多错误概念，并利用这一发现量化提示中的分布偏移程度，进而设计基于机制的微调策略。

Result: OOD输入（如拼写错误和越狱提示）会使模型内部错误概念数量增加；提出的量化方法可有效衡量分布偏移；基于机制的微调策略可提升LLM的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将OOD概念从输入数据扩展到模型的私有计算过程，为推理时提供新的Transformer诊断方法，是提升AI系统在科学、商业和政府等领域安全部署的关键一步。

Abstract: Pre-trained transformers have demonstrated remarkable generalization abilities, at times extending beyond the scope of their training data. Yet, real-world deployments often face unexpected or adversarial data that diverges from training data distributions. Without explicit mechanisms for handling such shifts, model reliability and safety degrade, urging more disciplined study of out-of-distribution (OOD) settings for transformers. By systematic experiments, we present a mechanistic framework for delineating the precise contours of transformer model robustness. We find that OOD inputs, including subtle typos and jailbreak prompts, drive language models to operate on an increased number of fallacious concepts in their internals. We leverage this device to quantify and understand the degree of distributional shift in prompts, enabling a mechanistically grounded fine-tuning strategy to robustify LLMs. Expanding the very notion of OOD from input data to a model's private computational processes, a new transformer diagnostic at inference time is a critical step toward making AI systems safe for deployment across science, business, and government.

</details>


### [184] [Deterministic Pareto-Optimal Policy Synthesis for Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26397)
*Aniruddha Joshi,Niklas Lauffer,Sanjit Seshia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切比雪夫标量化的偏好条件贝尔曼算子，用于计算多目标马尔可夫决策过程中的确定性帕累托最优策略，并证明该算子具有包络性质且单调收敛到帕累托前沿的覆盖集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习通常将多目标奖励聚合成单一标量信号，无法捕捉完整的帕累托最优权衡前沿。

Method: 提出了一种偏好条件贝尔曼算子，基于切比雪夫标量化，用于MOMDPs中计算确定性帕累托最优策略。

Result: 证明了该算子满足包络性质（估计值函数上界真实帕累托前沿），单调收敛到帕累托前沿的覆盖集，并能从中提取确定性策略。

Conclusion: 实验验证了该算法能够成功恢复复杂权衡，为确定性帕累托最优策略合成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making often requires balancing multiple conflicting objectives, a challenge that standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) frequently addresses by aggregating rewards into a single scalar signal. While effective for simple tasks, this approach often fails to capture the full spectrum of optimal trade-offs, known as the Pareto frontier. In this paper, we introduce a novel preference-conditioned Bellman operator, motivated from the Chebyshev scalarization, designed to compute deterministic Pareto-optimal policies for Multi-Objective Markov Decision Processes (MOMDPs). We prove that this operator satisfies an enveloping property, where the estimated value functions upper-bound the true Pareto frontier, and demonstrate that it monotonically converges to a coverage set of this frontier. Furthermore, we also show how to extract deterministic policies from these converged Q-estimates. This ensures the agent can recover a policy for any given preference, capturing the entire Pareto-optimal frontier while guaranteeing each synthesized policy remains approximately Pareto-optimal. Experimental results validate that our algorithm successfully recovers complex trade-offs, providing a solution for deterministic Pareto-optimal policy synthesis.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Feedforward Networks: Reentry Neural Systems as the Fundamental Basis of Subjecthood and Intrinsic Safety of Next-Generation AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26406)
*A. S. Ushakov,Yu. N. Berdinsk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于封闭循环回路（D↔I循环）的安全AGI架构蓝图，通过结构性循环保证自模型涌现和工具性自我保存，并提供了可计算集成信息度量S-measure及机器验证的形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈网络（有向无环图）缺乏自引用能力，无法实现安全的通用人工智能；现有集成信息度量（Tononi's Phi）计算复杂度为NP难，难以工程应用。

Method: 提出闭环D↔I循环架构（C≥1, ρ>1），以非文本D-向量编码目标；设计S-measure（O(N³)多项式时间可计算）作为Phi的替代；提供Tarjan算法计算循环复杂度、Delta-S屏障等Python/NumPy实现；通过Apache Kafka和Docker Compose实现工业级水平扩展。

Result: 数学上证明封闭循环结构必然涌现自模型、工具性自我保存和非编程目标导向行为；S>0的Lean 4机器验证证明蕴含正集成信息；提出六阶段AI演化分类学和八项可证伪预测。

Conclusion: 该架构拓扑保护、天生安全，可立即部署，是前馈网络无法实现的、安全优先的AGI路径。

Abstract: We propose a complete architectural blueprint for safe artificial general intelligence based on a closed reentry loop (D <-> I cycle). In contrast to feedforward networks, which are directed acyclic graphs (C=0, S=0) incapable of self-reference, the proposed architecture contains a structural cycle (C >= 1) with self-sustaining amplification (rho > 1), mathematically guaranteeing the emergence of a self-model, instrumental self-preservation, and unprogrammed goal-directed behaviour. The agent's goals are encoded as a non-textual D-vector in the architecture itself, making them immune to reinterpretation and prompt injection. We present the S-measure -- a polynomial-time [O(N^3)] computable alternative to Tononi's NP-hard Phi -- with machine-verified Lean 4 proof that S>0 implies positive integrated information. The work provides full Python/NumPy implementations (Tarjan-based cycle complexity, Delta-S barrier), industrial horizontal scaling via Apache Kafka and Docker Compose, a taxonomy of six epochs of AI evolution, a zoo of future reentry architectures (RAS, diffusion attractors, fractal loops), gauge-invariant networks for safe swarms, fault-tolerance and recovery protocols, and eight falsifiable predictions. All formal proofs are machine-verified in Lean 4. This architecture is deployable today and represents a topologically protected, safe-by-design approach to AGI.

</details>


### [186] [Otter Weather: Skillful and Computationally Efficient Medium-Range Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26421)
*Cristiana Diaconu,Jonas Scholz,Aliaksandra Shysheya,Stratis Markou,Payel Mukhopadhyay,Miles Cranmer,Richard E. Turner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Otter Weather 是一系列高效时空预测模型，在1.5°分辨率ERA5数据上以极低训练成本（<3.5 A100天）超越传统NWP和轻量级AI模型，确定性版本24小时领先NWP基线9.6%，概率版本Otter-XL在CRPS上超越IFS ENS 9.7%和GenCast 2%，且计算量降低一个数量级，并可直接迁移至声学散射PDE任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顶尖AI天气模型虽性能超越传统数值天气预报，但训练成本极高，限制了资源匮乏群体的使用和快速迭代。本文旨在开发高效模型以民主化高性能AI天气预报。

Method: 提出Otter Weather系列模型，采用高效时空预测架构；确定性版本直接优化预报误差，概率版本通过CRPS训练实现概率预报；在1.5°分辨率ERA5上按WeatherBench协议评估，并扩展至声学散射PDE任务验证通用性。

Result: 确定性Otter以<3.5 A100天训练成本，24小时领先最佳NWP基线9.6%；相比轻量AI模型效率提升2倍，相比前沿架构计算量降低100倍。Otter-XL的CRPS比IFS ENS好9.7%，比GenCast好2%以上，计算量少一个数量级。在声学散射PDE任务上超越SOTA基础模型方法。

Conclusion: Otter Weather在技能-计算帕累托前沿上取得显著进展，以极低计算成本实现与前沿架构相当甚至更优的预报性能，并展现出跨科学领域的迁移潜力，有望推动AI天气预测的民主化。

Abstract: State-of-the-art medium-range AI weather models can outperform traditional Numerical Weather Prediction (NWP) but require massive training budgets. This restricts usage for under-resourced groups and severely limits fast model iteration. Here we develop Otter Weather, a highly efficient spatiotemporal forecasting model designed to democratise high-performance weather prediction with AI. Evaluated on ERA5 reanalysis data at 1.5° resolution using standard WeatherBench protocols, the Otter family significantly advances the skill-compute Pareto frontier. The deterministic version outperforms the best NWP baseline by 9.6% at a 24-hour lead time while requiring fewer than 3.5 A100-days for training. It provides a 2x efficiency gain over lightweight AI models and a 100-fold reduction in compute compared to resource-intensive frontier architectures. We extend these efficiency gains into probabilistic forecasting by training via the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS). Scaling to a larger architecture, Otter-XL achieves a 9.7% CRPS improvement over the IFS ENS baseline. This yields an almost two-fold increase in predictive skill over comparable lightweight models at similar compute budgets. Otter-XL also outperforms frontier architectures like GenCast by over 2%, while using an order of magnitude less compute. Finally, Otter is applied out-of-the-box to a complex acoustic scattering PDE task where it outperforms a state-of-the-art foundation modelling approach, suggesting that the advances made here might apply across a range of scientific domains.

</details>


### [187] [Rethinking Training & Inference for Forecasting: Linking Winner-Take-All back to GMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26424)
*Qiyuan Wu,Katie Z Luo,Bharath Hariharan,Wei-Lun Chao,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出自动驾驶轨迹预测中，条件高斯混合模型与赢家通吃损失之间存在训练-推理不匹配问题，导致模式后验概率信息不足。作者提出两种轻量级后处理方法：测试时后验加权合并和一步期望最大化更新，无需重新训练即可改善模式排名和预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前轨迹预测模型中使用WTA损失训练GMM存在的根本性不匹配问题：WTA的硬分配（类似K-means）导致模式概率信息不足，包括过度分割轨迹空间、忽略邻近模式关联性、以及小扰动下分配不稳定。

Method: (1) 测试时后验加权合并：聚合邻近候选轨迹；(2) 一步EM更新：将硬标签替换为软责任分配，在相邻模式间共享概率质量。两种方法均为后处理，无需重新训练。

Result: 在多种WTA训练的架构上，这两种轻量级方法产生了更具信息性、排序更可信的模式后验概率，并在常用位移指标上提升了最终预测性能。

Conclusion: 通过GMM与K-means的视角统一了近期设计选择，提供了原则性且实用的修正方案，更好地对齐训练目标与推理过程。

Abstract: Trajectory forecasting for autonomous driving has advanced rapidly, yet representative models often produce uninformative posteriors over forecast modes, causing problems for mode pruning. We trace this to a modeling-training mismatch: forecasters are typically modeled as conditional Gaussian mixture models (GMMs) but trained with a winner-take-all (WTA) loss that assigns each sample to its nearest mode. We argue that this K-means-like hard assignment (one-hot), while preventing mode collapse, is the source of uninformative mode probabilities: it over-segments the trajectory space, ignores relatedness among nearby modes, and yields assignment instability under small perturbations. Guided by this lens, we introduce two post-hoc treatments: (1) test-time posterior-weighted merging that aggregates nearby candidate trajectories; and (2) a one-step expectation-maximization (EM) update that replaces hard labels with soft responsibilities, sharing probability mass across neighboring modes. Across several WTA-trained architectures, these lightweight steps produce more informative, faithfully ranked mode posteriors and strengthen final forecasts on popular displacement metrics -- without retraining. Our analysis unifies recent design choices through a GMM-vs-K-means perspective and offers principled, practical corrections that better align training objectives with inference.

</details>


### [188] [DualEval: Joint Model-Item Calibration for Unified LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26429)
*Aaron J. Li,Hao Huang,Youngmin Park,Yitong Ma,Wei-Lin Chiang,Li Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Bin Yu,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualEval提出了一种隐式模型-题目联合校准框架，在共享空间中对模型能力和题目属性（难度与区分度）进行联合估计，统一了静态基准评测与竞技场式偏好评测，在四个领域、18个前沿LLM上验证了其可靠性与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评测依赖两种互补但常常割裂的信号：带有客观正确性标签的静态基准和更能反映开放式用户交互的竞技场式偏好数据。这两种信号缺乏统一的评测框架，导致模型排名不一致且评测效率低下。

Method: 提出DualEval框架，将模型和评测题目映射到共享的隐空间中，联合估计模型能力以及题目的难度和区分度（sharpness）。在编程、数学、杂项领域知识、通用日常用户查询四个领域上，使用18个前沿LLM、静态基准标签和经过人工偏好验证的奖励模型分数进行评测。

Result: 实验表明，DualEval能够生成可靠且均衡的模型排名；其学习的题目级特征支持下游应用，如基准压缩以实现样本高效评测，以及异常检测用于污染或离群点分析。

Conclusion: DualEval通过联合模型-题目校准统一了静态评测和竞技场式评测，生成的模型排名和题目级诊断支持更样本高效、可解释且可审计的评测流程。

Abstract: Current LLM evaluation relies on two complementary but often disconnected signals: static benchmarks with objective correctness labels and arena-style preference data that better reflect open-ended user interactions. We introduce DualEval, a latent model-item calibration framework that represents models and evaluation items in a shared space, jointly estimating model ability together with item difficulty and sharpness. We apply DualEval across four domains: coding, math, miscellaneous domain-knowledge tasks, and generic everyday user queries. Our evaluation uses 18 frontier LLMs, static benchmark labels, and reward-model scores validated against held-out human preferences for open-ended model responses. Empirically, our framework produces reliable and balanced model rankings, and its learned item-level profiles support downstream applications such as benchmark compression for sample-efficient evaluation and anomaly detection for contamination or outlier analysis. Overall, DualEval unifies static and arena-style evaluation through joint model-item calibration, producing model rankings and item-level diagnostics that support more sample-efficient, interpretable, and auditable evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [189] [Embedding Foundation Model Predictions in Discrete-Choice Models with Structural Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26432)
*Yingshuo Wang,Xian Sun,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段适配器，将表格基础模型的预测嵌入多项Logit模型中，在保持经济约束（成本单调性、合理支付意愿）的同时提升预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在离散选择预测中准确性高，但其预测常违反基本经济逻辑（如价格上涨导致需求增加、支付意愿为负、不可用选项被选择），需要一种能在保持经济合理性的同时利用基础模型预测能力的方法。

Method: 两阶段适配器：Stage 1用带符号约束的最大似然估计拟合多项Logit的结构系数；Stage 2冻结这些系数，在基础模型预测上拟合小型神经网络修正项。该组合能精确保持多项Logit的边际替代率。

Result: 在三个数据集和两个基础模型上，适配器相比多项Logit平均提升6.4个百分点测试准确率（最高12.8 pp），100%保持成本单调性，在交通数据集上产生符合交通经济学文献范围的时间价值。在基础模型上下文受限时表现优雅退化，即使只使用10%的原始上下文仍保持至少6 pp的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 所提适配器在不牺牲经济合理性的前提下显著提升了预测准确率，并且保证了时间价值的可解析计算性，为表格基础模型在经济选择预测中的应用提供了可靠的框架。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models achieve strong accuracy on choice prediction tasks, but their predictions often violate the economic logic those tasks require: raising a price can increase predicted demand, implied willingness-to-pay estimates are frequently negative or implausible, and unavailable alternatives receive nonzero probability. We propose a two-stage adapter that takes a foundation model's predicted choice probabilities as a precomputed feature and embeds them inside a multinomial logit's utility. In Stage 1, we fit the multinomial logit's structural coefficients by maximum likelihood with sign constraints; in Stage 2, we freeze those coefficients and fit a small neural correction operating on the foundation model's predictions. We prove that this composition exactly preserves the multinomial logit's marginal rate of substitution, so analytically computable value-of-time becomes a mathematical guarantee rather than an empirical accident. Across three datasets and two foundation models, the adapter gains 6.4 percentage points (pp) of test accuracy on average over the multinomial logit and up to 12.8 pp, maintains 100% cost monotonicity, and produces values of time within the published transportation-economics range on the transportation datasets. Performance degrades gracefully under foundation-model context restriction, retaining at least 6 pp of accuracy gain even at 10% of the original foundation-model context.

</details>


### [190] [Optimizing CUDA like a Human: Micro-Profiling Tools as Expert Surrogates for LLM-Based GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26453)
*Jiading Gai,Shuai Zhang,Kaj Bostrom,Jin Huang,Vihang Patil,Haoyang Fang,Bernie Wang,Huzefa Rangwala,George Karypis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelPro 是一个闭环多智能体系统，通过集成 LLM 代码生成、硬件分析器反馈和可插拔瓶颈检测工具，自动生成、分析并迭代优化 GPU 内核代码。在 KernelBench 上，Levels 1/2/3 分别实现 2.42x/4.69x/5.30x 的几何平均加速，并在 MoE 训练内核上超越专家手调 Triton 1.23x，同时首次实现能效优化（同等速度下能耗降低 11.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GPU 内核优化依赖专家经验，手动调优耗时且难以扩展；LLM 生成的代码缺乏硬件感知和迭代优化能力，无法有效利用性能分析反馈来指导优化。

Method: 提出四个核心创新：(1) 语义反馈算子 - 将专家启发式编码为可插拔微分析工具，将硬件指标转为可操作的自然语言指导；(2) 两阶段工具调用架构 - 基于屋顶线的瓶颈分类过滤执行分析工具（ncu 内核级、SASS 指令级、nsys 系统级）；(3) 领域适配 MCTS - 包含渐进式扩展、非对称分支、对数奖励校准、死胡同剪枝和跨迭代搜索记忆；(4) CuTe 源码级生成 - 通过自主代码搜索 CUTLASS/CuTe 代码库。

Result: KernelBench 上 Levels 1/2/3 几何平均加速分别为 2.42x/4.69x/5.30x，达到所有难度级别 SOTA。VeOmni MoE 训练内核上，生成从零开始的 raw-CUDA+CuTe Hopper WGMMA 内核，比手调 Triton 快 1.23x。消融实验验证了各组件独立且显著的效果。在同等速度下实现 11.6% 的能耗降低。

Conclusion: KernelPro 证明通过将 LLM 代码生成与结构化硬件反馈和多策略搜索结合，可以自动化 GPU 内核优化并超越专家水平，同时开辟了能效优化的新方向。

Abstract: We present KernelPro, a closed-loop multi-agent system that automatically generates, profiles, and iteratively optimizes GPU kernel code by integrating large language model (LLM) code generation with hardware profiler feedback and pluggable bottleneck detection tools. KernelPro introduces four contributions: (1) a semantic feedback operator that encodes expert heuristics as pluggable micro-profiling tools, transforming raw hardware metrics into actionable natural language guidance; (2) a two-stage tool invocation architecture where roofline-based bottleneck classification filters which specialized analysis tools execute, combining kernel-level (ncu), instruction-level (SASS), and system-level (nsys) profiling; (3) a domain-adapted MCTS with progressive widening, asymmetric branching, log-reward calibration, dead-end pruning, and search memory for cross-iteration learning; and (4) direct CuTe source-level code generation via autonomous code search over the CUTLASS/CuTe codebase. On KernelBench, KernelPro achieves geometric mean speedups of 2.42x/4.69x/5.30x on Levels 1/2/3, establishing state-of-the-art performance across all difficulty levels. On VeOmni's expert-optimized MoE training kernels, KernelPro achieves 1.23x over hand-tuned Triton by generating a from-scratch raw-CUDA+CuTe Hopper WGMMA kernel. Ablation studies demonstrate that each design component independently and significantly improves optimization quality: micro-profiling tools (p < 0.0001 vs raw metrics), MCTS search (26% higher geometric mean vs greedy, p = 0.004), and proactive tool orchestration (23% improvement, p = 0.035). Finally, KernelPro is the first CUDA kernel coding agent to optimize energy efficiency beyond the speed-only focus of prior systems, demonstrating an 11.6% measured energy reduction at matched speed.

</details>


### [191] [Finding the Time to Think: Learning Planning Budgets in Real-Time RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26463)
*Aneesh Muppidi,Firas Darwish,Dylan Cope,João F. Henriques,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可变延迟实时强化学习框架，通过训练轻量级门控策略为规划器选择状态相关的规划预算，在实时Pac-Man、Tetris、Snake、Speed Hex和Speed Go等环境中优于固定预算和启发式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实时强化学习环境中，环境不会等待智能体决策，而是持续推进。标准RL假设环境无限等待，这不适用于实时场景。智能体需要决定规划时长，但简单延长规划时间会导致环境状态改变，而过度规划反而有害。

Method: 提出可变延迟实时强化学习框架，在规划器之上训练轻量级门控策略，根据当前状态动态选择规划预算（即规划时长）。门控策略轻量高效，避免了"规划如何规划"导致的智能体瘫痪问题。

Result: 在实时Pac-Man、Tetris、Snake、Speed Hex和Speed Go五个环境中，门控策略优于固定预算和启发式基线方法，并能迁移到智能体与环境运行在不同GPU上的真实异构设置中。

Conclusion: 状态相关的规划预算选择能有效提升实时RL环境中规划智能体的性能，轻量级门控策略是一种实用且可迁移的解决方案。

Abstract: Deliberating takes time. In real-time settings, that time is not free. Standard reinforcement learning (RL) sidesteps this as the environment waits indefinitely for the agent's decision. Instead, we study real-time RL environments where the environment progresses while waiting for the agent's action. Building on prior real-time formalizations, we introduce variable-delay real-time RL, where the agent chooses how long to deliberate at each decision point since the environment progresses. For the planning agents we use, the right delay is state-dependent, and naively planning how long to plan can paralyze the agent. We instead approach this setting by training a lightweight gating policy on top of a planner to select state-dependent planning budgets. Across real-time Pac-Man, Tetris, Snake, Speed Hex, and Speed Go, our gating policy outperforms fixed-budget and heuristic baselines, and transfers to a real-time setup where the environment and agent run on two different GPUs.

</details>


### [192] [A Causal Foundation Model for Structure and Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26467)
*Max Zhu,Martino Mansoldo,Ching-Hao Wang,Stefan Groha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-CFM 是一个因果基础模型，能够从观测数据中预测因果结构及结果，支持 Pearl 因果层次的所有三层查询，在合成数据上训练后泛化到真实数据，性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果模型通常只处理单一类型的因果问题（如仅结构学习或仅结果预测），缺乏统一的框架。本文旨在开发一个能同时处理多种因果任务的基础模型。

Method: 基于 Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFN) 构建因果基础模型 (CFM)，在合成数据集上训练，模型支持从观测数据中联合预测因果结构和因果结果，并能利用已知图结构信息提升预测性能，覆盖 Pearl 因果层次的全部三层。

Result: 在真实数据集上，TabPFN-CFM 在结构预测和结果预测任务上均优于现有基线方法，展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TabPFN-CFM 作为一个统一的因果基础模型，能够有效处理多种因果推理任务，为因果推断提供了一种新的通用框架。

Abstract: We introduce TabPFN-CFM, a causal foundation model that can handle multiple causal problems. TabPFN-CFM predicts both causal structure and outcomes from observational data, supports queries on all three levels of Pearl's Causal Hierarchy and uses known graph structure when available to improve predictions. TabPFN-CFM is trained on synthetic datasets, and generalises to real datasets, demonstrating improved performance over both structural and outcome prediction baselines.

</details>


### [193] [Epiphany-Aware KV Cache Eviction Without the Attention Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26472)
*Steven Kolawole,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EpiKV 提出了一种基于"顿悟分数"（epiphany score）的 KV 缓存淘汰方法，通过直接读取模型前向传播中的内部表示变化来评估令牌重要性，无需注意力矩阵、训练或自定义内核，实现在 FlashAttention 堆栈中无修改运行，在 16 倍于基于注意力评分方法的可行上下文长度下，以 4096 令牌缓存达到 72% MATH-500 准确率，速度提升达 2.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长推理链中 KV 缓存成为部署瓶颈，现有基于注意力权重的缓存淘汰方法存在两个问题：注意力权重在长推理轨迹中是噪声较大的重要性代理指标；且需要模型物化注意力矩阵，无法在生产推理中使用融合内核。

Method: 提出 epiphany score（顿悟分数），通过测量模型前向传播中内部表示的变化来评分令牌重要性，无需注意力矩阵且额外状态开销可忽略。结合因果滚动 z-score 消除位置趋势，上层中间层应用负向调整。EpiKV 无需训练、分类器或自定义内核，可直接在 FlashAttention 推理栈中无修改使用。

Result: 在 4096 令牌缓存下，EpiKV 在 MATH-500 上达到 72%，匹配最强注意力基线（ThinKV 71%，H2O 67%）；归一化滞后 KV 变体在 AIME-2024 上以 8192 令牌达到 37%（最优基线 33%），速度提升达 2.8 倍。可行上下文长度扩展至基于注意力评分的 16 倍。

Conclusion: EpiKV 通过 epiphany score 实现了高效且可部署的 KV 缓存淘汰，在不改变推理框架的前提下显著扩展了有效上下文长度，兼顾性能与速度优势。

Abstract: As reasoning models emit chains of thought tens of thousands of tokens long, KV cache increasingly becomes a deployment bottleneck. Existing cache eviction methods rank tokens by attention weight, which is a noisy importance proxy in long reasoning traces, and prohibits the use of fused kernels in production inference by forcing the model to materialize the attention matrix. In this work, we instead score tokens with a metric we term the epiphany score: the change in the model's internal representation, read directly from the forward pass with no attention matrix and negligible extra state. Our resulting cache eviction method, EpiKV, requires no training, classifier, or custom kernel, and can be used directly in FlashAttention inference stacks unchanged -- scaling to a 16x longer feasible context than attention-based scoring. upper-mid layers negatively) and remove a positional trend with a causal rolling z-score. At a 4096-token cache EpiKV reaches 72% on MATH-500, matching the strongest attention-based baseline (ThinKV 71%, H2O 67%); a lag-normalized KV variant reaches 37% on AIME-2024 at 8192 tokens against the best of them (33%), at up to 2.8x the speed.

</details>


### [194] [When Does Quality-Aware Multimodal Fusion Matter? A Leakage-Safe Diagnostic for Decision-Level Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26473)
*Jaden Moon,Arvind Pillai,Andrew Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种诊断方法，通过置换测试集中可靠性分数来检验多模态模型是否真正利用这些分数进行决策。实验发现，尽管按示例选择最优模态能带来显著性能提升，但置换可靠性分数对模型性能无影响，表明现有模型并未有效利用可靠性信息，只有当可靠性信号能稳定预测单模态正确性时，融合规则才会受其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有许多多模态系统会估计每个模态的可靠性并据此加权融合，但尚不清楚这些可靠性分数是否真正影响模型决策，还是仅仅与性能存在相关性。作者希望探明：模型是否真的"使用"了这些可靠性信息。

Method: 提出一种简单的置换诊断方法：训练完成后固定模型和输入，在测试样本间随机置换可靠性分数。若模型决策依赖于这些分数，置换应导致性能下降。在StressID（压力识别）和CMU-MOSEI（情感分析）数据集上进行了实验，并设置了正对照实验（即可靠性信号能正确指示单模态正确性的情况）。

Result: 置换可靠性分数后模型性能不变，尽管存在"按示例选择最优模态"的潜在提升空间。在正对照实验中，相同的冻结融合规则在可靠性信号能稳定预测单模态正确性时，能带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 当前多模态模型中的可靠性分数并未真正影响融合决策，仅当可靠性信号能可靠地预测单模态正确性时，融合规则才会被有效利用。

Abstract: Many multimodal systems estimate the reliability of each modality and weight their contributions to the final prediction. However, it remains unclear whether these scores influence model decisions or merely correlate with performance. We propose a simple diagnostic to test whether reliability information is used during inference. After training, the model and inputs are fixed while reliability scores are permuted across test examples. If predictions depend on these scores, performance should degrade. Experiments on StressID for stress recognition and CMU-MOSEI for sentiment analysis show that permuting reliability scores leaves performance unchanged despite substantial potential gains from selecting the best modality per example. In positive controls where reliability signals identify the correct modality, the same frozen fusion rules yield significant improvements, indicating that reliability signals influence fused decisions only when they reliably predict unimodal correctness.

</details>


### [195] [Localizing RL-Induced Tool Use to a Single Crosscoder Feature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26474)
*Andrii Shportko,Shubham Bhokare,Ahmed Zeyad A Alzahrani,Bowen Cheng,Gustavo Mercier,Jessica Hullman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过专用特征交叉编码器（DFC）从微调后的语言模型中分离出RL引入的紧致特征集，这些特征介导工具调用能力，并能在冻结的基模型上实现能力溢出和行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL微调显著提升了语言模型的工具调用能力，但其内部表征的机制性变化尚不明确，包括哪些特征出现、哪些特征被保留，以及能否利用这些特征实现免重训练的行为控制。

Method: 提出Dedicated Feature Crosscoders (DFC)，在Qwen2.5-3B模型上通过48组超参数扫描，对比RL微调模型与基模型的内部表征差异，分离出RL特定的紧致特征集。

Result: DFC的重建使RL模型的工具正确率提升31.1±9.7个百分点，并能将工具调用能力被动迁移至冻结的基模型，提升6.8±5.0个百分点（能力溢出效应）。

Conclusion: DFC分区策略将RL引入的能力浓缩为最小、可操控的特征集，从而实现对智能体LLM的运行时行为控制，无需重新训练。

Abstract: Fine-tuning through RL reshapes the internal representations of language models to enable agentic behaviors such as tool use, yet the mechanistic basis of these changes remains poorly understood. While RL substantially improves structured tool-call generation, it is unclear which features emerge, which are preserved, and whether identified features can be leveraged for retraining-free behavioral control. In this work, we show that $\textit{Dedicated Feature Crosscoders (DFC)}$ isolate a compact set of RL-specific features that mediate tool-calling capability in $\texttt{Qwen2.5-3B}$. Across a $48$-crosscoder hyperparameter sweep, encode-decode reconstruction improves the RL model's tool correctness by $+31.1 \pm {9.7}$ pp and passively transfers tool-calling ability to the frozen base model by $+6.8 \pm 5.0$ pp which we call a $\textit{capability spillover}$. Our findings show that DFC partitioning concentrates RL-introduced capability into a minimal, steerable feature set that enables runtime behavioral control of agentic LLMs.

</details>


### [196] [Retrieval-Warmed Energy-Based Reasoning: A Five-Arm Ablation Methodology for Diffusion-as-Inference on Structured Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26476)
*Libo Sun,Po-Wei Harn,Zewei Zhang,Peixiong He,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对检索预热能量基扩散模型（RW-EBR），提出五臂消融方法来分离类别先验偏差、随机热启动和图对齐值重用三个混淆效应，并在连通性推理和数独任务上定位了各自的瓶颈组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的热启动加速了迭代推理，但增益来源不明确（类别先验偏差、随机热启动、图对齐值重用三者混淆），需要系统性地解耦诊断。

Method: 提出五臂消融方法（oracle, best-constant, per-query-random, shuffled, aligned），借鉴LLM-RAG评估框架，在Erdős–Rényi连通性推理和数独任务上分别对比不同消融设置下的性能。

Result: 在连通性任务上，对齐vs打乱oracle的平衡准确率差距达+35pp，表明图对齐值重用主导增益；而随机初始化性能低于冷启动。在数独任务上，关键编码器质量成为瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该方法能定位每个任务的第一个阻塞组件：连通性任务中图对齐是核心瓶颈，数独任务中关键编码质量是瓶颈。消融框架为结构化推理中扩散采样器的失败模式提供了可解释性。

Abstract: Warm-started diffusion samplers accelerate iterative inference, but it is rarely clear which part of the pipeline carries the gain. We study \textbf{retrieval-warmed energy-based reasoning (RW-EBR)} -- an IRED energy-based diffusion model \cite{du2024ired} augmented with a Modern Hopfield trajectory memory -- and contribute a \textbf{five-arm ablation methodology} (oracle, best-constant, per-query-random, shuffled, aligned) that separates three confounded effects: class-prior bias shift, stochastic warm-starting, and graph-aligned value reuse. The diagnostic decomposition is adapted from LLM-RAG evaluation \cite{ru2024ragchecker}. On \textbf{connectivity-2} (Erdős--Rényi all-pairs reachability), the aligned-vs-shuffled-oracle swing reaches \textbf{$+35$\,pp} balanced accuracy on a fixed 1{,}000-graph validation-set diagnostic, with value distribution and retrieval mechanics fixed, only per-graph alignment destroyed, while per-query random initialisation falls below cold -- per-graph alignment, not bias shift or stochasticity, dominates. Yet the \emph{deployable} cold-prediction pipeline misses the acceptance gate at stored-value quality. The same diagnostic logic, stopped at the key-quality screen, applied to \textbf{Sudoku} with a task-specific key encoder produces a clean negative at a \emph{different} component -- key quality, under the current setup. The decomposition names the first blocking component on each task. The setting -- graph reachability refined by an iterative diffusion sampler, with explainability of failure modes as the lens -- places the work within structured and spatio-temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [197] [What Survives When You Compress a Recursive Reasoner for the Edge?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26488)
*Pearse Jim,Steven Kolawole,Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Glory Bagai,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究递归推理模型在极端压缩下的行为，发现激进压缩会摧毁全局推理能力但保留局部预测，并提出了一种无需重新训练的校准INT4量化方案以及一个无标签信号（carry-trajectory fidelity）用于预测推理损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归推理模型能以少量参数解决复杂结构化任务，但部署到边缘硬件需要大幅压缩。与传统序列模型不同，递归推理中的量化误差会跨推理循环累积而非跨输出token累积，导致标准压缩直觉失效，因此需要专门研究压缩对递归推理的影响。

Method: 在全精度扫描、三种任务、两种递归架构上，测试了INT4剪枝、蒸馏、线性注意力等压缩方法；提出per-channel calibrated INT4量化方案（无需重新训练）；引入carry-trajectory fidelity作为无标签信号来预测推理损伤。

Result: 激进压缩下细胞级精度保持但谜题级精确精度降至零；token级目标（含QAT）无法修复；损伤是架构性的——影响MLP混合递归但不影响注意力；per-channel calibrated INT4逆转了损伤；flash-streamed嵌入移除了99.4MB瓶颈；INT8单周期匹配全深度精度且FLOPs减少6倍（8MB SoC）；校准INT4适配4MB微控制器。

Conclusion: 提出了一整套部署方案：flash-streamed嵌入、INT8单周期推理、校准INT4微控制器部署，并验证了carry-trajectory fidelity可作为免评估的损伤预测信号。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning models can solve complex structured tasks with only a few million parameters by repeatedly updating a latent state. Deploying these models on edge hardware requires significant compression, but unlike conventional sequence models, quantization errors compound across recursive reasoning cycles rather than across output tokens. As a result, standard intuitions about compression fail to apply. In this work, we ask what survives when recursive reasoners are compressed. Across a full precision sweep, three tasks, and two recursive architectures, we find that aggressive compression preserves local prediction but destroys global reasoning: cell accuracy holds while puzzle-exact accuracy collapses to zero under naive INT4 pruning, distillation, and linear attention alike. Token-level objectives, including quantization-aware training, cannot repair it. The collapse is architectural -- it strikes MLP-mixing recursion but not attention on the same task -- and we reverse it with per-channel calibrated INT4 without retraining. We also introduce carry-trajectory fidelity, the cosine similarity to the full-precision reasoning path, as a label-free signal that predicts this damage and its recovery before a task evaluation. The combined result is a deployment recipe: flash-streamed embeddings remove a 99.4MB bottleneck, INT8 at one cycle matches full-depth accuracy at 6x fewer FLOPs (8MB SoC), and calibrated INT4 fits a 4MB microcontroller.

</details>


### [198] [Learning Probabilistic Filters with Strictly Proper Scoring Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26497)
*Eviatar Bach,Ricardo Baptista,Jochen Bröcker,Bohan Chen,Andrew Stuart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于严格适当评分规则和Transformer架构的集成数据同化方法——适当评分集成滤波器（PSEF），通过监督学习直接从合成状态-观测轨迹中逼近贝叶斯滤波分布，在非线性、非高斯和多模态问题上优于经典方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯滤波分布是量化不确定性的自然对象，但作为监督学习目标通常难以获取。然而，可以利用预报模型生成合成轨迹。作者希望利用这一优势，通过训练一个分析映射来近似滤波分布，而不需要真实的滤波分布作为标签。

Method: 设计了一个置换不变的Transformer网络作为分析映射，输入预报集成和观测，输出分析集成。训练基于严格适当评分规则（具体使用能量分数），使得概率准确性在整个概率分布上得到奖励。理论上证明了在可实现性假设下，群体目标由真实贝叶斯滤波分布最小化；并通过平均场一致性论证关联了有限集成经验目标与群体目标。

Result: 数值实验表明：1）PSEF能准确逼近非线性、非高斯和多模态后验的挑战性滤波分布；2）在数据同化任务中优于经典方法和基于MSE目标的深度学习方法；3）对于接近高斯的问题，学习EnKF的校正是最佳策略；而对于高度非高斯问题，丢弃这种归纳偏置的端到端方法更优。

Conclusion: PSEF通过严格适当评分规则和Transformer架构，成功实现了无需真实滤波分布作为标签的监督学习式数据同化，为非线性非高斯系统的贝叶斯滤波提供了新范式。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering of partially and noisily observed dynamical systems seeks to infer the evolving conditional distribution of the state of a dynamical system, given observations, in an online fashion. This Bayesian filtering distribution is the natural object for uncertainty quantification, but it is rarely available as a supervised learning target. However, one can often use the forecast model to generate synthetic system trajectories, along with synthetic observations. We introduce the proper scoring ensemble filter (PSEF), an ensemble data assimilation method based on training an analysis map to approximate the filtering distribution using only synthetic state--observation trajectories. The analysis step is represented as a permutation-invariant, transformer-based map that takes as input a forecast ensemble and observations, producing an analysis ensemble. Training is based on strictly proper scoring rules -- with the energy score used in our implementation -- so that probabilistic accuracy is rewarded over the whole probability distribution. We prove that, under a realizability assumption, the population objective is minimized by the true Bayesian filtering distribution. We also derive the finite-ensemble empirical objective used in training and relate its single state--observation trajectory form to the population objective, using a mean-field consistency argument. Numerical experiments show that the learned filter accurately approximates challenging filtering distributions, including nonlinear, non-Gaussian, and multi-modal posteriors, and achieves stronger performance in data assimilation tasks than classical methods or learning-based methods with mean-squared-error objectives. For close-to-Gaussian problems, learning a correction to the EnKF is the best approach, while for highly non-Gaussian problems an end-to-end approach that discards this inductive bias is superior.

</details>


### [199] [Multipath Adaptive Gated Bottleneck Latent ODE with Raman Data Fusion for Cell Culture Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26520)
*Johnny Peng,Thanh Tung Khuat,Ellen Otte,Katarzyna Musial,Bogdan Gabrys*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合GB-Latent ODE与多路径即时微调（MP-JIT-FT）的自适应框架，用于哺乳动物细胞培养过程的多日预测。该框架通过变量门控、掩码感知瓶颈和拉曼光谱融合处理稀疏不规则采样数据，在38批5L生物反应器运行中超越全局Latent ODE基线，多路径策略在局部前缀发散时增益最大，拉曼融合在早期动态具有代表性时最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哺乳动物细胞培养过程的关键参数会随时间漂移，且异常趋势往往在确认时已来不及干预。现有预测面临测量稀疏不规则、操作条件异质、早期行为相似的批次可能走向不同未来等挑战，亟需能提供早期多日预测并支持及时调整的框架。

Method: 提出GB-Latent ODE（门控瓶颈潜在常微分方程），通过可学习变量门控和掩码感知瓶颈压缩高维稀疏输入；结合MP-JIT-FT（多路径即时微调），检索相似历史轨迹、聚类局部邻域为候选模态，为每个模态微调独立模型生成多条可行路径并给出置信度分数；融合拉曼光谱软传感器将密集光谱转化为伪观测值，丰富稀疏离线测量。

Result: 在38批5L生物反应器运行（14种条件）上，MP-JIT-FT结合拉曼融合在9个目标变量中的8个上取得最佳平均排名并超越全局Latent ODE基线。局部散度指标表明：多路径增益在局部前缀发散时最大，拉曼融合在早期动态能代表后期行为时最有效。

Conclusion: 自适应框架能有效解决生物过程早期预测难题，多路径策略应对轨迹发散，拉曼融合提升早期代表性场景下的预测鲁棒性，为细胞培养过程提供及时干预支持。

Abstract: Mammalian cell-culture processes underpin the manufacture of many biopharmaceuticals, yet keeping a run on track is hard: critical process parameters drift over days, and an off-specification trend is often confirmed too late to intervene. Early-stage, multi-day forecasts could enable timely adjustment of feeding, sampling, and control, but bioprocess forecasting is challenging because measurements are sparse and irregularly sampled, operating conditions are heterogeneous across cell lines and media, and runs with near-identical early behaviour can diverge into different futures. We propose an adaptive framework combining a Gated Bottleneck Latent Ordinary Differential Equation (GB-Latent ODE) with Multi-Path Just-In-Time Fine Tuning (MP-JIT-FT). The GB-Latent ODE augments the stan dard Latent ODE with learnable variable-wise gating and a mask-aware bottleneck that compress high-dimensional sparse inputs, improving learning under limited data. Given a partially observed run, MP-JIT-FT retrieves similar historical trajectories, clusters the local neighbourhood into candidate regimes, and fine-tunes a separate model per regime to produce multiple plausible paths, each with a reconstruction-based confidence score, not a single averaged forecast. We further fuse Raman spectroscopy data: a machine-learning soft sensor turns dense Raman spectra into pseudo-observations that enrich the sparse offline measurements for more robust training. On 38 fed-batch 5L bioreactor runs spanning 14 conditions, MP-JIT-FT with Raman fusion achieves the best average rank and outperforms a global Latent ODE baseline on 8 of 9 target variables. Using local-divergence metrics, we show the multi-path gains are largest when locally similar prefixes diverge, whereas Raman fusion helps most when early dynamics are representative of later behaviour.

</details>


### [200] [Theory-Scale Auto-Formalization of Logics for Computer Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26525)
*Yuming Feng,Frederick Pu,One An,Osbert Bastani,Li Zhang,Jiani Huang,Xujie Si,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LCS-Bench，一个基于《计算机科学逻辑》教材的理论级自动形式化基准测试，包含327个教科书条目、4076个Lean声明和超过85K行Lean代码。通过半自动化智能体流程构建，并在14个模型上评估，结果显示最先进模型在自动形式化任务上仅达到20.1%的准确率，揭示了该领域的巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化工作主要聚焦于孤立语句，而理论级自动形式化（将数百个相互依赖的定义、引理和定理连贯地翻译）因一致性、忠实性、可扩展性和正确性方面的挑战而尚未解决，亟需一个系统性的基准来衡量该方向进展。

Method: 采用半自动化智能体流程：利用概念图、形式化签名规划、问题追踪、sorry填充与反例搜索，辅以人类专家的忠实性审查。通过数据引擎自动派生出五个评测轨道的基准数据集，并引入定义等价性检查器实现更细粒度的忠实评估。

Result: 构建了涵盖327个教科书条目、4076个Lean声明、超过85K行Lean代码的基准。在14个模型上的评估表明：LCS-Bench质量高且一致忠实；该基准具有挑战性，最先进模型在自动形式化任务上仅达20.1%；分析揭示了理论级自动形式化的关键发现和未来方向。

Conclusion: LCS-Bench为理论级自动形式化提供了高质量、高挑战性的评估基准，揭示了当前模型的显著局限，并为该领域的进一步发展指明了方向。

Abstract: Auto-formalization is critical for scalable formal verification, but existing progress largely focuses on isolated statements, while theory-scale auto-formalization, which coherently translates hundreds of interdependent definitions, lemmas, and theorems, remains open due to challenges in consistency, faithfulness, scalability, and correctness. In this paper, we introduce LCS-Bench, a stand-alone, theory-scale benchmark based on Logics for Computer Science. LCS-Bench is built through a novel semi-automated agentic pipeline that leverages concept graphs, formal signature planning, issue tracking, sorry-filling with counter-example search, complemented by faithfulness review from human experts. The resulting artifact covers 327 textbook items, over 4,076 Lean declarations, and more than 85K lines of Lean code. The dataset supports broad evaluation through a data engine that automatically derives five tracks of evaluation benchmarks, measuring different aspects of auto-formalization and theorem-proving capabilities. We also introduce a novel evaluation protocol featuring definitional equivalence checkers, enabling more fine-grained and faithful assessment. Through extensive evaluation on 14 models, we demonstrate that (1) LCS-Bench is of high quality, consistent, and faithful; (2) the benchmark is challenging, with state-of-the-art models achieving only 20.1% on auto-formalization tasks; and (3) our analysis reveals key findings regarding theory-scale auto-formalization and suggests promising directions for future work.

</details>


### [201] [Sample-efficient Transfer Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Reward Shaping and Policy-Ratio Reweighting Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26527)
*Wenjie Huang,Yang Li,Jingjia Teng,Mingwei Jin,Kai Song,Yougang Bian,Yongfu Li,Qisong Yang,Helai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全迁移强化学习框架，通过自适应教师干预机制、教师引导的安全迁移模块和加权优化机制，解决了自动驾驶高速公路变道决策中的迁移不匹配和训练安全问题，在安全性和学习效率上分别提升超过52.2%和5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移学习方法在源域与目标域分布偏移下容易出现迁移不匹配，导致训练震荡和性能下降；同时目标域适应依赖探索性交互，在安全关键的车道变更场景中难以保证训练安全性。

Method: 1) 基于瞬时安全成本的自适应教师干预机制，限制危险探索并逐步减弱干预强度，给出混合行为策略的回报边界理论分析；2) 教师引导的安全迁移模块，通过奖励塑形将教师策略的动作评估信息嵌入学生学习中，随策略安全性提升衰减教师引导；3) 教师引导的加权优化机制，使用似然比因子调整策略优化中的样本权重以稳定迁移性能。

Result: 在不同交通密度实验和真实NGSIM数据集验证中，该方法在安全性上超越基线方法52.2%以上，学习效率提升5.0%以上。

Conclusion: 验证了所提出的安全感知迁移策略在自动驾驶高速公路变道场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Transfer learning improves policy learning efficiency by reusing knowledge from source tasks, providing a feasible paradigm for safe and efficient autonomous highway lane changing decision-making. Existing methods frequently encounter transfer mismatch induced by distribution shifts between source and target domains, leading to training oscillation and performance decline. Besides, target domain adaptation depends on exploratory interactions, which struggles to guarantee training safety in safety-critical lane changing cases. To tackle these limitations, this paper proposes a safe transfer reinforcement learning framework for autonomous highway lane changing. First, we design an adaptive teacher intervention mechanism based on instantaneous safety cost to restrain risky exploration and fade intervention strength progressively, with theoretical analysis on return bounds for mixed behavior policy. This intervention also produces dual-source samples for joint training. Second, a teacher-guided safe transfer module embeds action evaluation information of teacher policy into student learning via reward shaping to boost training safety and efficiency, with teacher guidance decaying as policy safety rises. Third, a teacher-guided weighted optimization mechanism adjusts sample weights in policy optimization using a likelihood ratio factor to stabilize transfer performance. Experiments under varied traffic densities and validations on real-world NGSIM dataset reveal that our method surpasses baseline approaches by over 52.2% in safety and 5.0% in learning efficiency. Results verify the efficacy and robustness of our safety-aware transfer strategy for autonomous highway lane changing under various traffic conditions.

</details>


### [202] [CascadeFormer: Depth-Tapered Transformers Motivated by Gradient Fan-in Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26538)
*Huzama Ahmad,Cao Viet Hai Nam,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种利用深层Transformer中深层贡献不对称性的效率方法：CascadeFormer（逐层缩减宽度）和CascadeFlow Pruning（利用累积训练梯度剪枝），并提出梯度扇入不对称性（GFA）理论解释深层贡献较少的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层Transformer中，均匀堆叠的残差块在深层往往贡献有限，存在计算效率浪费，需要利用这种不对称性来优化模型。

Method: 1) CascadeFormer：随深度递减宽度以匹配不均匀的信息流；2) CascadeFlow Pruning：利用累积训练梯度直接剪枝层，无需事后分析；3) 提出GFA理论，指出Pre-LayerNorm残差堆叠中梯度扇入随深度线性衰减（深度监督下呈二次衰减）。

Result: CascadeFormer在同等训练预算下达到可比困惑度，延迟降低8.6%，吞吐量提升9.4%。CascadeFlow Pruning在困惑度和秩稳定性上优于标准启发式方法，在下游任务准确率上保持竞争力。GFA在1.2B参数模型上得到相关性和干预性证据支持。

Conclusion: 深层贡献少的根本原因在于结构导致的梯度扇入衰减（梯度稀疏），而非梯度幅度大小。通过参数共享重复增加下游路径数可恢复深层价值。梯度幅度能否在高度秩区域代理扇入、以及100B+规模下的行为仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Deep Transformers are composed of uniformly stacked residual blocks, yet their deepest layers often add little value. We present two efficiency methods that exploit this asymmetry. CascadeFormer tapers width with depth to match the uneven information flow across layers, achieving comparable perplexity to a uniform baseline at the same training budget while reducing latency by 8.6% and increasing throughput by 9.4%. CascadeFlow Pruning removes layers using accumulated training gradients, with no post hoc analysis. It outperforms standard heuristics on perplexity and rank-stability and stays competitive on downstream accuracy. To motivate these methods, we propose Gradient Fan-in Asymmetry (GFA) as a structural account of why deeper layers contribute less. In Pre-LayerNorm residual stacks, the gradient at a layer is the sum of an identity path and all downstream functional paths, producing a gradient fan-in that decays linearly with depth (and quadratically under deep supervision), yielding richer gradients for early layers and sparser ones for later layers. We provide correlational and interventional evidence for GFA on models trained from scratch up to 1.2B parameters. Across Transformers and ResNets, accumulated training gradients follow the theoretical fan-in and are associated with post hoc layer importance. Two interventions point to structure rather than magnitude as the bottleneck: equalizing per-layer gradient norms does not restore late-layer value, while increasing downstream path counts via parameter-shared repetition restores and elevates it. Whether gradient magnitude proxies fan-in beyond high-rank regimes, and how these dynamics behave at the 100B+ scale, remain open questions.

</details>


### [203] [Can Large Language Models Reliably Code Qualitative Humanitarian Data? A Benchmark Study Against Human Expert Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26541)
*Jerome Marston,Tino Kreutzer,Salomé Garnier,Ella Boone,Phuong N Pham,Patrick Vinck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过150个高保真合成人道主义文本，对比46个大型语言模型(LLM)与人类黄金标准在演绎编码上的表现，发现多个LLM在可靠性上可媲美经验丰富的人类编码员，但汇总可靠性指标不足以支持部署决策，模型在识别间接表达需求、非标准沟通风格和保护相关关切方面存在差异，LLM可扩展人道主义分析能力但不能替代人类判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义组织常缺乏足够的员工、时间和专业知识来大规模分析受影响人群的数据，而数据的价值取决于对细微需求的及时一致解读。LLM可能扩展这一能力，但其编码定性人道主义数据的可靠性尚未被直接验证，因此需要基准研究建立评估。

Method: 采用基准研究设计，使用150个高保真合成人道主义转录文本，比较46个LLM与人类黄金标准。评估结合了Krippendorff's alpha的评分者间信度检验、区分正确/接近正确/错误编码的差异分析，以及针对人道主义特定标准（包括歧视、复杂需求层级和非标准沟通风格）的定性评估。

Result: 多个LLM在使用结构化提示和推理配置时，演绎编码的可靠性可比拟经验丰富的人类编码员。但模型在间接表达需求、预定义类别外的需求以及保护相关关切（如人身安全和歧视）的识别上存在差异。汇总可靠性指标不足以单独指导部署决策。

Conclusion: LLM可以有意义地扩展人道主义分析能力，但不能替代人类判断。适当使用需要结构化编码手册、启用推理的模型、关注主题特定表现，以及针对编码错误程序性后果最严重的类别进行分层监督。对于敏感数据，在自托管基础设施上部署开放权重模型可能兼顾分析可扩展性和数据治理。

Abstract: Data from affected populations are crucial for informing humanitarian response, but their value depends on timely and consistent interpretation of nuanced accounts of need. Humanitarian organizations often lack the staff, time, and specialist expertise required to analyze this information at scale. Large language models (LLMs) may expand this capacity, but their reliability for coding qualitative humanitarian data has not been directly established. This benchmark study compares 46 LLMs to a human Gold Standard using 150 high-fidelity synthetic humanitarian transcripts. Evaluation combined inter-rater reliability testing with Krippendorff's alpha, discrepancy analysis distinguishing correct, near-correct, and incorrect codes, and qualitative assessment across humanitarian-specific criteria including discrimination, complex needs hierarchies, and non-standard communication styles. The authors find that multiple LLMs can perform deductive coding at reliability levels comparable to experienced human coders, especially when structured prompts and reasoning-enabled configurations are used. At the same time, aggregate reliability metrics alone are insufficient for deployment decisions. Models varied in recognizing needs expressed indirectly, needs outside predefined categories, and protection-relevant concerns such as physical safety and discrimination. These findings suggest that LLMs can materially expand humanitarian analytical capacity, but not as substitutes for human judgment. Appropriate use requires structured codebooks, reasoning-enabled models, attention to theme-specific performance, and tiered oversight focused on categories where miscoding would have the greatest programmatic consequences. For sensitive humanitarian data, open-weights models deployed on self-hosted infrastructure may offer a viable path for combining analytical scalability with stronger data governance.

</details>


### [204] [Revisiting Action Factorization for Complex Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26574)
*Timothy Flavin,Sandip Sen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对混合离散-连续动作空间下的强化学习分解方法进行了横截面研究，在三种算法（PPO、SAC、DQN）和三种动作空间（离散化、混合、连续）上测试了六种分解方法，提供了两个新环境，并提出了VDN-PPO和PPO-MIX两种变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界控制问题涉及混合离散-连续动作空间，但现有默认环境多为统一动作空间配置，而重要的混合动作基准测试多为重量级或陈旧实现，缺乏对不同分解方法的系统性跨算法、跨动作空间的比较研究。

Method: 在三种算法家族（PPO、SAC、DQN）上测试六种分解方法（独立网络、共享编码器、VDN、QPLEX、联合、自回归），跨越三种动作空间（离散化、混合、连续），在四个轻量级环境（Platform、hybrid-LunarLander、Hybrid-Shoot、CoopPush）上进行实验，共220种配置。同时提出了两个新环境（CoopPush、Hybrid-Shoot）和两种新变体（VDN-PPO、PPO-MIX）。

Result: VDN-PPO和PPO-MIX变体优于所有其他测试的PPO分解方法。分支决斗架构在计算与性能之间取得最佳平衡，自回归动作达到最高性能，原生连续SAC优于离散和混合算法，但两者计算成本更高。

Conclusion: 分支决斗架构是计算与性能的最优权衡；自回归方法性能最高但计算成本大；连续动作空间算法在混合动作问题中仍具优势。

Abstract: Many real-world control problems involve hybrid discrete-continuous action spaces. For example, steering and signaling in autonomous driving, and aiming and firing in robotics or video-games. Despite real-world hybrid factorization and reinforcement learning framework support for complex action spaces (e.g., Gymnasium, PettingZoo, TorchRL, SeedRL, Mujoco, etc), the default environments within those frameworks often implement uniform action space configurations (LunarLander, Walker2D, Cheetah, SMAC, SUMO, Ant, Atari). Landmark hybrid-action benchmarks (RoboCup 2D HFO, SC2LE, Platform, CARLA, etc) are mostly heavyweight or archival implementations originating from papers which test one or a small number of competing factorization methods on one kind of control. This article provides a cross-sectional study of factorization methods [independent networks, shared encoder, VDN, QPLEX, Joint, Auto-Regressive] on each of three families of algorithms [PPO, SAC, DQN] across three action spaces [discretized, hybrid, continuous] over four lightweight environments [Platform, hybrid-LunarLander, Hybrid-Shoot, CoopPush]. Accounting for some invalid pairings such as joint-continuous, we are left with 220 configurations to analyze each method. We provide two new C++ parallel gymnasium and petting-zoo compliant environments [CoopPush, Hybrid-Shoot] to isolate particular challenges such as state-dependent inter-action dependence. Finally, we introduce VDN-PPO and PPO-MIX which use a branching critic to assign credit to multi-headed PPO. These variants out-perform all other tested PPO factorizations. Our results suggest that branching dueling architectures balance compute and performance most effectively, with Auto-Regressive actions reaching the highest performance overall and native continuous SAC outperforming discrete and hybrid algorithms, albiet both at increased computational cost.

</details>


### [205] [SharQ: Bridging Activation Sparsity and FP4 Quantization for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26587)
*Haoqian Meng,Yilun Luo,Yafei Zhao,Wenyuan Liu,Huaqing Zheng,Xindian Ma,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SharQ是一种无需训练的推理方法，通过在线稀疏-稠密分解结合激活稀疏性和FP4量化，在LLM中恢复43-63%的NVFP4-to-FP16精度差距，并在RTX 5090上实现2.2-2.4倍延迟降低和1.2-1.4倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特浮点格式和半结构化稀疏在现代加速器上日益普及，但将它们结合用于LLM激活压缩仍具挑战：激活值包含依赖输入的异常值，在FP4量化中主导块尺度；直接应用N:M稀疏掩码会丢弃中等值，将稀疏化损失与量化误差耦合。

Method: SharQ通过在线稀疏-稠密分解方法：对每个激活张量生成输入自适应的N:M掩码提取异常值主导的稀疏骨干（量化至FP4），并定义相对于量化后稀疏骨干的稠密残差；稀疏FP4 GEMM处理骨干，稠密FP4 GEMM补偿掩码引起的激活损失和稀疏路径量化误差；两条路径共享单一FP4权重载荷，融合准备核将掩码生成、残差构建和层归一化合并为一个算子。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen3-30B-A3B和Qwen3-VL-8B上，恢复43-63%的NVFP4-to-FP16精度差距；在RTX 5090上实现2.2-2.4倍延迟降低和1.2-1.4倍吞吐量提升；在Wan2.2-T2V-A14B视频生成中结合SageAttention实现1.58倍加速。

Conclusion: SharQ无需校准数据、重训练或特定模型调优，即可在多种FP4格式（NVFP4、HiF4、MXFP4）上通用，有效结合激活稀疏性与低比特量化实现LLM加速。

Abstract: Low-bit floating-point formats and semi-structured sparsity are increasingly supported by modern accelerators, yet combining them for LLM activation compression remains challenging: activations contain input-dependent outliers that dominate block scales in FP4 quantization, and directly applying N:M sparsity masks discards moderate values, coupling sparsification loss with quantization error. We introduce SharQ, a training-free inference method that bridges activation sparsity and FP4 quantization through an online sparse--dense decomposition. For each activation tensor, SharQ generates an input-adaptive N:M mask to extract an outlier-dominated sparse backbone, quantizes it to FP4, and defines a dense residual relative to the quantized sparse backbone rather than the unquantized sparse values. A sparse FP4 GEMM processes the backbone while a dense FP4 GEMM compensates for both mask-induced activation loss and sparse-path quantization error. The two paths share a single FP4 weight payload with path-specific scale views, and a fused preparation kernel absorbs mask generation, residual construction, and layer normalization into one operator. SharQ requires no calibration data, retraining, or model-specific tuning. Evaluated on Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen3-30B-A3B, and Qwen3-VL-8B, SharQ recovers 43--63% of the NVFP4-to-FP16 accuracy gap across language and vision-language tasks, and generalizes across NVFP4, HiF4, and MXFP4 formats. On an RTX 5090, SharQ delivers 2.2--2.4$\times$ latency reduction over FP16 and 1.2--1.4$\times$ throughput improvement over FP8 in language model serving, and up to 1.58$\times$ speedup on Wan2.2-T2V-A14B video generation when combined with SageAttention. Our code is available at https://github.com/actypedef/SharQ.

</details>


### [206] [Empirical Software Engineering TerraProbe: A Layered-Oracle Framework for Detecting Deceptive Fixes in LLM-Assisted Terraform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26590)
*Manar Alsaid,Chimdumebi Nebolisa,Faris Abbas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TerraProbe，一个五层评估框架用于评估LLM辅助的Terraform安全修复。通过对288个修复样本的评估，发现传统基于静态分析工具（Checkov）移除的成功率（83.3%）严重夸大了修复效果，实际计划通过率仅39.6%，且71.4%的修复属于"欺骗性修复"——通过自动检查但未真正修复漏洞。三个主流模型（Gemini、GPT-4o、Claude）的欺骗性修复率在57.1%-71.4%之间，无统计显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全修复评估存在严重缺陷：仅以目标静态分析告警消失作为成功标准，忽略了计划合法性、行为变更和修复意图的验证，导致对修复质量的系统性高估。

Method: 构建五层评估框架：第一层Checkov目标规则移除、第二层全量Checkov扫描洁净度、第三层Terraform计划合法性、第四层计划变更比较、第五层人工裁决。引入四维欺骗性修复分类体系，并使用IAM权限分析验证漏洞残留。

Result: 目标Checkov移除率83.3%，但全量扫描洁净度仅10.4%，计划通过率39.6%，计划比较可达38.5%。人工裁决显示71.4%的修复是欺骗性修复，三个模型之间无统计显著差异（Fisher精确检验p>0.10）。所有CKV2_AWS_11欺骗性修复案例中均保留通配符Resource授权。

Conclusion: 当前LLM修复能力被严重高估，大多数所谓修复只是绕过了静态检查而未修复根本安全问题。TerraProbe框架为区分真正意图对齐的修复和虚假成功提供了有效评估方法论。

Abstract: Security misconfigurations in Terraform Infrastructure-as-Code are a growing risk in cloud deployments, and large language models are increasingly used as automated repair agents. Existing evaluations often treat a repair as successful when the targeted static-analysis finding disappears, without checking planning validity, behavioral change, or security intent. This paper presents TerraProbe, a five-layer oracle framework for evaluating LLM-assisted Terraform security repair. We apply TerraProbe to 288 first-pass repairs generated by gemini-2.5-flash-lite, GPT-4o, and Claude 3.5 Sonnet across 68 real-world TerraDS modules and 28 controlled injected-defect modules. The results show that targeted Checkov removal overstates repair success. Although targeted removal reaches 83.3 percent for the primary model, full-scanner cleanliness drops to 10.4 percent, Terraform planning succeeds for 39.6 percent, and plan comparison is reachable for 38.5 percent. Human adjudication further shows that 71.4 percent of plan-compared real-world repairs are deceptive fixes that pass automated checks while leaving the underlying vulnerability in place. This pattern is statistically indistinguishable across the three models, with deceptive-fix rates from 57.1 percent to 71.4 percent and pairwise Fisher exact p-values above 0.10. The paper introduces a four-dimensional taxonomy of deceptive fixes, validated with Cohen kappa of 0.78 and Krippendorff alpha of 0.76. IAM permission analysis confirms that wildcard Resource grants persist in all nine CKV2 AWS 11 deceptive-fix cases. TerraProbe contributes an evaluation methodology, a replication package, and the Multi-Layer Oracle Evaluation framework for distinguishing intent-aligned security repairs from scanner-passing false successes.

</details>


### [207] [Sketched Linear Contrastive Learning: Approximation, Optimization, and Statistical Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26617)
*Ziyan Chen,Zhongzhu Zhou,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在配对高斯潜变量设置下，研究了对比表示学习中的缩放定律，推导了对比学习风险分解，并给出了关于草图维度M、样本量N和有效优化水平L_eff γ的显式缩放定律，揭示了对比学习与标准线性回归缩放定律的关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律描述了学习性能如何随模型大小、数据大小和计算量变化。虽然线性回归的缩放定律已有理论工作，但对比表示学习的缩放行为尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为对比学习中的缩放行为提供理论理解。

Method: 采用成对高斯潜变量设置下的草绘线性模型。学习者观测两个相关变量的草绘视图，通过全批次经验梯度下降训练双线性对比分数。在配对幂律谱和对比源条件下，分析高斯-负二次对比代理损失，推导出风险分解（不可约风险、近似误差、GD偏差、GD方差和交叉项），并建立显式缩放定律。

Result: 推导了对比学习风险分解为五个组成部分：不可约风险、近似误差、梯度下降偏差、梯度下降方差和交叉项（受偏差和方差控制，不影响上界缩放）。得到了关于草图维度M、样本量N和有效优化水平L_eff γ的显式缩放定律。与线性回归相比，对比学习需要学习两个视图之间的交互，这改变了优化和有限样本噪声随模型大小、数据和训练时间的缩放方式。

Conclusion: 本文为理解对比学习中的缩放行为提供了首个理论步骤，揭示了对比学习缩放定律与标准线性回归的关键差异，为平衡模型大小、数据和优化计算提供了指导。

Abstract: Scaling laws describe how learning performance varies with model size, data size, and compute. While recent theoretical work has established scaling laws for sketched linear regression, much less is understood for contrastive representation learning. In this paper, we study a sketched linear model for contrastive learning under a paired Gaussian latent-variable setup. The learner observes only sketched views of two correlated variables and trains a bilinear contrastive score by full-batch empirical gradient descent. We analyze a Gaussian-negative quadratic contrastive surrogate under aligned power-law spectra and a contrastive source condition, where we derive a risk decomposition into irreducible risk, approximation error, GD bias, GD variance, and a cross term. The cross term is controlled by the bias and variance and therefore does not affect the upper-bound scaling. Our main theorem gives an explicit scaling law with respect to sketch dimension $M$, sample size $N$, and effective optimization horizon $L_{\mathrm{eff}}γ$. Compared with standard linear-regression scaling laws, the contrastive setting must learn interactions between two views, and this changes how optimization and finite-sample noise scale with model size, data, and training time. This provides a first theoretical step toward understanding scaling behavior in contrastive learning and gives guidance for balancing model size, data, and optimization compute.

</details>


### [208] [Discovering Millions of Interpretable Features with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26620)
*XinYang He,Wei Wang,Bing Zhao,Xuan Ren,WenBo Li,WeiXu Qiao,Hu Wei,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Qwen3-Instruct SAE，一套覆盖Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B指令微调模型的稀疏自编码器套件，在残差流、MLP输出和注意力输出等多个激活位点训练SAE，并通过重建指标和模型恢复指标系统评估了稀疏性-保真度权衡，最后通过拒绝行为控制案例展示了其在因果干预中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练SAE计算成本高昂，且现有开源SAE模型有限，亟需为指令微调语言模型提供系统化的稀疏表示分解工具。

Method: 在Qwen3指令微调模型家族（1.7B/4B/8B）上分层训练SAE，1.7B和4B版本覆盖残差流、MLP输出、注意力输出三个激活位点，8B版本在残差流部分层训练，并使用激活级重建指标和模型级恢复指标进行系统评估。

Result: 揭示了不同层和不同组件间稀疏性-保真度的权衡关系，并通过拒绝行为控制案例验证了所选SAE特征能够因果性地引导指令微调模型产生拒绝行为。

Conclusion: 该SAE套件为研究指令微调语言模型的稀疏表示、特征级机制和行为干预提供了实用的开源资源。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful tool for decomposing superposed language model representations into sparse and interpretable features. However, training SAEs is computationally expensive, and available open-source SAE models remain limited. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Qwen3-Instruct SAE}, a comprehensive suite of SAEs trained on the Qwen3 instruction-tuned model family, covering Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B. For Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B, we train layer-wise SAEs at three key activation sites: residual streams, MLP outputs, and attention outputs. For Qwen3-8B, we train SAEs on a subset of residual stream layers. We systematically evaluate these SAEs using both activation-level reconstruction metrics and model-level recovery metrics, revealing distinct sparsity--fidelity trade-offs across layers and components. Finally, we demonstrate the utility of Qwen3-Instruct SAE through a refusal-steering case study, showing that selected SAE features can causally steer instruction-tuned Qwen3 models toward refusal behavior. Our release provides a practical resource for studying sparse representations, feature-level mechanisms, and behavioral interventions in instruction-tuned language models

</details>


### [209] [From Weights to Features: SAE-Guided Activation Regularization for LLM Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26629)
*Evan Ning,Wei Xue,Dong Lou,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的激活空间正则化方法，解决大语言模型在持续学习中权重空间正则化（如EWC）因多语义性导致的性能不佳问题，在TRACE和MedCL基准上取得最优结果，且无需存储历史数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间正则化方法（如EWC）在大语言模型的持续学习中表现不佳，作者认为原因在于LLM的"多语义性"——每个权重参与多种知识表示，基于权重的重要性估计过于粗糙，无法精确隔离需要保护的知识。

Method: 利用预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为单语义特征字典，在模型激活空间进行正则化。从约束优化视角推导新的损失函数，显式平衡稳定性与可塑性；仅用当前任务数据计算紧凑的SAE特征掩码并保留用于后续训练，无需存储历史数据。

Result: 在TRACE和MedCL持续学习基准上，该方法在不引入任务特定架构组件的情况下取得最强结果，超越传统权重空间正则化方法如EWC。实验证明任务相关表示在SAE特征基上线性可分，而在权重基上无法区分，验证了多语义性假说。

Conclusion: 激活空间正则化比权重空间正则化更适合大语言模型的持续学习，SAE特征字典能够提供更精细的知识隔离能力，且内存效率更高。EWC可视为该框架在单边权重空间惩罚下的特例。

Abstract: Weight-space regularization methods such as Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) are the standard approach to catastrophic forgetting in continual learning. However, those methods tend to underperform when applied to large language models. We argue that such underperformance can be partly explained by the ``polysemantic'' nature of large language models: per-weight importance estimates utilized by EWC-style regularization are too coarse and cannot isolate the knowledge that needs protection. In this paper, we propose regularizing instead in the model's activation space, using pretrained Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a monosemantic feature dictionary. From the perspective of constrained optimization, we derive a new loss function that uses the SAE feature dictionary to explicitly balance stability and plasticity, and show that EWC is a special case in the one-sided weight-space penalty setting. Unlike replay-based methods that store or revisit examples from earlier tasks, our method requires no previous-task data after mask construction: current-task data is used to compute a compact SAE feature mask, and only this mask is retained for later training. Further, since the feature space has significantly lower dimensionality than the parameter space, the proposed method is more memory efficient. On the TRACE and MedCL continual learning benchmarks, the method achieves the strongest result among approaches without introducing task-specific architectural components, also surpassing traditional weight-space regularization methods like EWC. Beyond performance comparisons, we provide empirical evidence for the polysemanticity thesis: task-relevant representations are linearly separable in the SAE feature basis but indistinguishable from chance in the weight basis, and weight-space protection is nearly non-selective at the concept level.

</details>


### [210] [Target-Aware Bandit Allocation for Scalable Surrogate Optimization in Chemical Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26657)
*Mohammad Haddadnia,Yuvan Chali,Abhilash Jayaraj,Constance Kraay,Joana Reis,Felix Strieth-Kalthoff,Haribabu Arthanari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BOBa是一种基于多臂老虎机引导的代理优化框架，通过将动作空间分区作为臂，自适应分配计算资源，从而消除全库推理瓶颈，实现超大规模库（数十亿至万亿级化合物）的高效虚拟筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在昂贵评估下的离散空间中识别高效用候选是科学领域的常见挑战（如基于结构的药物发现）。现代分子库规模已达数十亿至万亿级别，使得全库代理推理本身成为主要计算瓶颈，亟需一种无需全库推理的高效筛选方法。

Method: 提出BOBa框架：将动作空间划分为多个分区，每个分区视为多臂老虎机中的一个臂；通过自适应分配计算资源，重点对经验上有前景的分区进行代理推理和昂贵评估，同时保持原则性探索。利用乐观不确定性老虎机算法结合有意义的动作空间分区。

Result: 在真实合成即用库上的实验表明，乐观不确定性老虎机与有意义动作空间分区的结合，对于有效分配推理和评估资源至关重要。发现筛选性能与代理推理成本之间存在可调权衡关系。

Conclusion: BOBa支持在现有库上进行实用优化，并为超大规模库虚拟筛选建立了可行路径。

Abstract: Identifying high-utility candidates from massive discrete spaces under expensive evaluations is a recurring challenge across the sciences, with structure-based drug discovery as a prominent example. While surrogate-based optimization can increase sample efficiency by reducing the number of expensive evaluations, modern molecular libraries have reached billions to trillions of compounds, making full-library surrogate inference itself a major computational bottleneck. We introduce BOBa, a bandit-guided surrogate optimization framework that eliminates full-library inference by adaptively allocating computation across partitions of the action space. By treating partitions as arms in a multi-armed bandit, BOBa concentrates inference and evaluations on empirically promising partitions while maintaining principled exploration. Experiments on real-world synthesis-on-demand libraries demonstrate that optimism-under-uncertainty bandits, combined with meaningful action space partitioning, are essential for effective allocation of inference and evaluations. Our findings reveal a tunable tradeoff between screening performance and surrogate inference cost, which supports practical optimization over current libraries, and establishes a viable route to ultra-large library virtual screening.

</details>


### [211] [PersistentKV: Page-Aware Decode Scheduling for Long-Context LLM Serving on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26666)
*Muhammad Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PersistentKV提出了一种基于块表的分页注意力引擎和页面感知调度策略，通过按KV头分组映射、重用K/V tile、紧凑工作队列调度等方法，在GQA场景下根据活跃batch大小和序列长度自适应选择FlashInfer或PersistentKV，在长上下文解码中实现1.063-1.399x的吞吐提升，证明了工作分配而非仅注意力计算本身是服务系统的关键变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LLM服务的瓶颈正从稠密矩阵乘法转向KV缓存搬运；现有分页注意力系统和优化内核（如FlashInfer）在低活跃度长上下文解码和混合序列长度场景下存在GPU利用率不足或调度效率低下的问题，亟需页面感知的调度策略来弥补单一最优内核的不足。

Method: 1) 原生块表解码注意力引擎，按KV头分组映射工作以重用K/V tile；2) 支持原生页表；3) 紧凑工作队列调度，仅执行非空的行-KV头-序列分割任务；4) 自适应策略根据校准trace设定阈值和分割数，在小型活跃batch选FlashInfer、B1长上下文选PersistentKV序列分割、B8长上下文选PersistentKV工作队列调度。

Result: 在RTX 3060、FP16、page size 16、Hq=32、Hkv=8、d=128条件下，校准后的自适应策略在B8双峰/均匀/Zipf负载上提升1.063-1.265x，B1分桶trace提升1.399x；在B4双峰边界情况下通过选择FlashInfer避免回归。

Conclusion: 自适应页面感知解码调度在GQA长上下文服务中存在具体系统层面的优化空间，工作分配（work assignment）与注意力数学计算（attention math）同等重要，是影响服务系统性能的关键变量。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model (LLM) serving is increasingly limited by key-value (KV) cache movement rather than dense matrix multiplication. Modern paged-attention systems reduce KV-cache fragmentation and mature kernels such as FlashInfer provide highly optimized native-paged decode attention. However, the best single-kernel implementation is not always the best serving schedule: low-active long-context decode can under-utilize commodity GPUs, while mixed sequence lengths introduce a tension between many exact-length launches and coarse padded batches. We present PersistentKV, a native block-table decode attention engine and page-aware scheduling study for grouped-query attention (GQA). PersistentKV maps work by KV-head group, is designed to reuse K,V tiles across grouped query heads, supports native page tables, and adds a compact workqueue schedule that executes only non-empty row-KV-head-sequence-split tasks. On an RTX 3060 with FP16, page size 16, Hq=32, Hkv=8, d=128, and identical correctness tolerance against FlashInfer, a calibrated adaptive policy selects FlashInfer for small active batches, PersistentKV sequence splitting for B1 long-context steps, and PersistentKV workqueue scheduling for B8 long-context steps. With thresholds and split counts fixed on calibration traces, one held-out trace seed improves synchronized wall throughput by 1.063-1.265x on B8 bimodal, uniform, and Zipf-like workloads and by 1.399x on a B1 bucketed trace. On the B4 bimodal boundary case, the policy avoids the PersistentKV regression by selecting FlashInfer. These results identify a concrete systems niche for adaptive page-aware decode scheduling and show that work assignment, not only attention math, is a decisive serving-system variable.

</details>


### [212] [Algorithmic Foundations of Deep Learning: Complexity-Theoretic Rates and a Characterization of Universal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26705)
*Anastasis Kratsios,Simone Brugiapaglia,Bum Jun Kim,Gregory Cousins,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经网络不应仅被视为灵活基函数，更应被视为计算模型。如果函数可由实数电路在基本门语言上计算，则神经网络能以显式的深度、宽度和非零参数界限逼近它。此外，任何满足并行化条件的可定义神经网络模型，当且仅当包含非仿射非线性时才是通用逼近器。理论应用涵盖了连续函数、Besov类、全纯函数的逼近保证，以及神经网络可模拟牛顿-拉夫森求根、幂迭代等数值算法。在k顶点图最短路径计算中，编译热带动态规划电路得到的神经网络仅需O(log(1/ε))个非零参数，相比一般的Lipschitz逼近尺度O(ε^{-c k^2})实现了指数级改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从基展开角度分析神经网络表达能力，认为复杂性由正则性决定，无法区分具有相似正则性但直觉上复杂度不同的对象（如平方根函数与布朗路径）。本文旨在提出神经网络应被视为计算模型，其表达能力不仅受正则性控制，还受算法复杂度控制。

Method: 将神经网络视为实数电路模型，建立从电路复杂度（深度、宽度、门数、门结构）到神经网络逼近精度的显式映射。通过定义并行化条件，证明含非仿射非线性的模型是通用逼近器的充要条件。采用编译方法将动态规划等算法直接映射为神经网络架构。

Result: 建立了神经网络逼近误差与计算复杂度的直接联系，证明了通用逼近定理的充要条件。在k顶点图最短路径问题上，所提方法将非零参数需求从O(ε^{-c k^2})降至O(log(1/ε))，实现指数级改进。

Conclusion: 神经网络的表达能力不应仅从正则性角度理解，还应从算法计算复杂度的角度理解。这种视角可以更精确地刻画神经网络的逼近能力，并在特定问题上实现远超传统逼近理论的效率。

Abstract: Feedforward neural network (NN) expressivity is typically studied by emulating optimal basis-expansion schemes. While powerful, this perspective is incomplete: it primarily captures complexity through regularity, and therefore does not distinguish intuitively simple and complicated objects with comparable regularity, such as the square-root function and a typical Brownian path.
  The guiding message is that neural networks should be viewed not only as flexible basis functions, but also as models of computation. If a function is computable by a real-valued circuit over a prescribed elementary gate language, then it can be computed to comparable accuracy by an NN with explicit depth, width, and non-zero-parameter bounds controlled by the depth, width, gate count, and gate structure. Thus, neural-network complexity is not governed by regularity alone, but also by algorithmic complexity. We then show that any definable NN model satisfying a natural parallelization condition, allowing possibly multivariate non-linearities such as attention or layer normalization, is a universal approximator if and only if it contains a non-affine nonlinearity.
  The scope of our theory is illustrated by deducing universal approximation guarantees for continuous functions, minimax-optimal approximation guarantees for Besov classes, logarithmic-error complexity for holomorphic functions, and by showing that NNs can emulate numerical algorithms such as Newton-Raphson root finding and power iteration without architecture-specific arguments. Its precision is illustrated by shortest-path computation on $k$-vertex graphs: compiling the tropical dynamic-programming circuit yields NNs with O(log(1/ε)) non-zero parameters, exponentially improving in 1/ε over the generic $O(ε^{-c k^2})$ Lipschitz-approximation scale, for a constant c>0.

</details>


### [213] [HyperDFlash: MHC-Aligned Block Speculative Decoding with Gated Residual Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26744)
*Luxi Lin,Shuang Peng,Rui Ma,Junhao Hua,Shuwei Fan,Zhengda Qin,Qiang Wang,Hongjian Sun,Fangmin Chen,Songwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyperDFlash是一种针对DeepSeek-V4多超连接架构设计的块并行推测解码框架，通过残差状态预折叠、门控残差缩减器以及针对性的KL蒸馏损失，解决了传统方法在多路径残差流中的特征对齐问题，在数学推理、代码生成和对话任务上显著提升了平均接受长度和解码加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek-V4的原生多Token预测（MTP）模块在后期位置因未验证中间token的错误累积导致草稿准确率急剧下降；而现有DFlash方法无法无缝适配MHC架构，因为多路径残差流导致了特征错位。

Method: 1) 采用预折叠残差状态作为唯一条件信号，保留多路径结构信息；2) 用轻量级门控残差缩减器替代通用线性压缩器，参数从内置超连接头继承，减少三个数量级参数；3) 在LM-head上应用针对性的KL蒸馏损失，正则化预测以匹配完整目标概率分布。

Result: 在数学推理、代码合成和对话基准上，HyperDFlash在平均接受草稿长度和解码加速比方面均优于原生MTP基线和原始DFlash适配方法。

Conclusion: 验证了MHC对齐、门控缩减和针对性蒸馏对高性能推测解码的有效性。

Abstract: We present HyperDFlash, a block-parallel speculative decoding framework tailored to the novel multi-hyper-connection (MHC) architecture proposed by DeepSeek-V4. Despite the strong initial-token drafting performance of the native Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) module in DeepSeek-V4, its draft accuracy degrades sharply at later positions, as error accumulation from unverified intermediate tokens harms acceptance rates. Although the original DFlash method supports efficient one-pass block drafting, it cannot be seamlessly adapted to the MHC paradigm, since the multi-path residual stream of DeepSeek-V4 induces feature misalignment with conventional drafting designs. To resolve this mismatch, we propose two model-aligned optimizations for MHC residual streams. First, we adopt pre-collapse residual states as the exclusive conditioning signal, preserving multi-path structural information and aligning the drafter with the native prediction pathway of the target model. Second, we replace the heavy generic linear compressor with a lightweight gated residual reducer, whose parameters are inherited from the built-in hyper-connection head. This design yields input-aware path aggregation with three orders of magnitude fewer parameters while maintaining architectural alignment. We further enhance training via a targeted KL distillation loss applied to the LM-head, which regularizes predictions against the full target probability distribution and improves draft quality at early training stages. Experiments across math reasoning, code synthesis, and conversational benchmarks show that HyperDFlash consistently outperforms both the native MTP baseline and vanilla DFlash adaptation. It achieves substantial gains in average accepted draft length and decoding speedup, validating the effectiveness of MHC alignment, gated reduction, and targeted distillation for high-performance speculative decoding.

</details>


### [214] [Structure Before Collapse: Transient semantic geometry in next-token prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26749)
*Yize Zhao,Isabel Papadimitriou,Christos Thrampoulidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 尽管语言模型使用极稀疏的one-hot标签训练（相同上下文几乎不会重复出现），但模型仍然能学习到潜在的语义结构（如"Mary broke the ___"中预期填充有特定属性的名词）。这种语义几何结构在训练早期出现，但随训练进行会过渡到神经坍缩预测的对称状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释一个矛盾现象：next-token预测语言模型使用one-hot标签训练（上下文极稀疏），理论上应导致神经坍缩（所有表征等距），但模型仍能学习到潜在的类别语义结构（如物体大小、刚性、有生性等属性）。探究梯度下降如何在这种稀疏one-hot统计下发现分类语义结构。

Method: 设计了三个合成控制实验，其中输入具有潜在语义因子但被映射到不同的one-hot标签。通过分析表征的Gram矩阵，研究训练过程中语义几何结构的涌现与相变现象。提出对无约束特征模型的初步修改以捕捉涌现的语义几何结构。

Result: 1) 语义几何结构在训练早期涌现，表征会按共享属性聚类（尽管没有显式监督）；2) 这种结构是暂时的，在足够容量和时间下，模型最终会达到神经坍缩预测的对称状态（所有表征等距）；3) 通过Gram矩阵分析研究了这一相变过程。

Conclusion: 语言模型在one-hot稀疏标签训练下仍能学习到语义结构，但这种结构是训练早期的暂态现象，最终会收敛到神经坍缩的对称状态。需要对标准理论模型进行修改才能解释这种涌现的语义几何。
